parāmbāyāḥ prasādānugrahābhyāṃ, mīmāṃsaka mūrdhanyena paritoṣa miśreṇa praṇī-
tayā 'jitayā' samṛddhasya tantravāttikasya dvitīyādhyāyātmakaṃ dvitīyaṃ bhāgaṃ yathā-
mati pariśodhya viduṣāṃ samakṣaṃ prastuvatāmasmākaṃ manasi mahānānandaḥ paritoṣaśca
sañjāyete |
varṣadvayāt prāgevāsyā ajitāyāḥ prathamo bhāgaḥ prakāśatāmagamat sudhobhiḥ
samādṛtaśceti saubhāgyamasmākamiti manye | prasiddhapāṇḍityāḥ ḍā0 viśva-
nārāyaṇaśāstrī asamaprāntataḥ, pro0 śrī anantalāla ṭhākura mahābhāgaḥ vaṅga
prāntataḥ, ḍā0 ena. esa. rāmānujatātācāryaḥ tirupati kṣetrataḥ, ḍā0 muralīdhara
pāṇḍeyaḥ vārāṇasītaśca sampādaka prakāśako prati likhitāḥ śubhāsaṃśā etadarthaṃ
preṣayāmāsuriti svakīyaṃ śramaṃ vayaṃ saphalaṃ manyāmahe | tān vidvadvareṇyān prati
kṛtaveditāṃ ca vinivedayāmaḥ | nāsmākamātmaguṇo'tra prabhavati kintu bhagavato
viśveśvarasyānugrahā divya vibhūtermahātmanaḥ ma0 ma0 ḍā0 sara gaṅgānātha jhā
mahābhāgasya ca śubhāśīrvādā evātra nimittaṃ vidyate |
'siddhayanti mahatsvapi karmasu yanniyojyāḥ |
sambhāvanāguṇamavehi
tamīśvarāṇām |' iti
mahākaveḥ kālidāsasya vacaḥ śaraṇamihāsmākam | uttara pradeśa saṃskṛta
akādamī saṃsthāyā lakhanau sthitāyā api kṛtajñāḥ vayam, yayā vividhapuraskā-
rāntargateneyamapi rūpyakasahasradvaya mitena puraskāreṇa sammānitā |

( 2 )
prathamabhāgasyāsyāḥ prastāvanāyāṃ mātṛkāstantravāttikaṭīkā: śāstra
paramparārāścādhikṛtya yathāmati taduktaṃ yadvivakṣitamāsīt | atra puna: dvādaśa
lakṣaṇyā asyā mīmāṃsāyā vivecya viṣayaparicayaḥ yathāmati saṃkṣepeṇa prastūyate |
C
mīmāṃsā dvādaśalakṣaṇī [ dvādaśādhyāyātmikā ] ṣoḍaśalakṣaṇī [ ṣoḍaśā
dhyāyātmikā] veti vicikitsā hetu: dvādaśādhyāya gatāntimasūtrasya tatrāvṛttyā
pāṭhaḥ | sūtrāvṛttiḥ samāpti sūcayatīti vedāntasūtreṣu dṛṣṭam | ata evā-
trāpi tathā kalpitam | anyathā kiṃ prayojanikā tatrāntimasūtrāvṛttiriti
jijñāsāyā asamāhitatvāt | kiñca prāyaḥ vākyārthanirṇayopayogino viṣayā
api dvādaśādhyāyāntargatā eva samupalakṣyante | saṅkarṣakāṇḍātmakeṣu caturvvadhyāyeṣu
kvacidvedavākyeṣu prāgavicāritainyayai ra nirṇeyārthatāmālakṣya tannirṇayopayogino
nyāyā nirūpitāḥ | atra vyavasthitakramo nāsti kintu saṅkīrṇāḥ santi viṣayā
iti heto: saṃkarṣa iti nāma |
atra catuścatvāriṃśadadhikasaptaśatottarāṇi dvisahasramitāni sūtrāṇi
vidyante | pratyadhyāyaṃ catvāraḥ santi pādāḥ kadācidaṣṭāvapi pādā bhavantyadhyāye |
sa hi śarabhapādanāmnā prasiddhayatyadhyāyaḥ | yato hi śarabhanāmnaḥ pakṣi-
viśeṣasyāṣṭo bhavanti pādāḥ | pratyekaṃ pādaḥ punaradhikaraṇeṣu vibhakto'sti |
vivādaviṣayastāvadihādhikaraṇapadavācyatāṃ yāti | vivādasya nirṇayāya
pañcāṅgāni prakriyātvenātra nirdhāritāni santi-saṅgatiḥ, viṣayaḥ, saṃśayaḥ,
pūrvapakṣa: siddhāntaśceti |
viṣayo
viśayaścaiva
pūrvapakṣastathottaram |
saṅgatiśceti pañcāṅgaṃ śāstre'dhikaraṇaṃ smṛtam |
iti padyaṃ prasiddhameva |
mādhavasya jaiminīyanyāyāmālā, maṇḍana miśrasya mīmāṃsānukramaṇikā
cādhikaraṇapādādhyāyānāmavagatyai nitarāmupakuruta: | atraivābhihitam-
dharmo dvādaśakṣaṇyā vyutpādyastatra lakṣaṇaiḥ |
pramāṇabhedaśeṣatvaprayuktikramasaṃjñakāḥ

( 3 )
adhikāro'tideśaśca sāmānyena viśeṣataḥ |
ūho bādhaśca tantraśca prasaṅgaścoditaḥ kramāt ||
atra dvādaśalakṣaṇyā mīmāṃsayā dharmaḥ lakṣaṇaiḥ vyutpādya ityuktam | ata eva
ko dharmaḥ, kiṃ lakṣaṇo'so, kānyasya sādhanānīti jāyate jijñāsā | lakṣaṇa-
rvyutpādya ityasya tātparyaṃ tu laghunopāyena viṣayāvabodhaḥ upāyāntareṇa na
sambhavati | yena viṣayasya yathārthaṃ svarūpamavagamyate tallakṣaṇaṃ bhavati, itara
vyāvartakadharmavatvaṃ hi lakṣaṇasya lakṣaṇam |
manye dvisahasramiteṣu sthaleṣu tadānīṃ vivāda āsīt | ata eva dvisahasrā-
ṇyadhikaraṇāni prasiddhapāṇḍityena mādhavena jaiminīyanyāyamālāyāmuddiṣṭāni
vidyante |
purākāle prāyaḥ guruparamparātaḥ parijñātakarmakāṇḍāḥ sarve janāḥ yajña-
yāgādikarmasu dhurīṇā evābhūvan | ata eva dharmaṃsūtrādīnyanusṛtya svasvakarmasu
pravṛttāḥ kṛtakṛtyatāmagacchan | paścāt janeṣu kramaśaḥ hrāsatāṃ gate karmakāṇḍajñāne
parivartite ca samaye alpajñeṣu karmakāṇḍamadhikṛtya bahudhā samutpannaḥ vivādaḥ
mīmāṃsāśāstramāvirakarot | tatkālaṃ ye vivādaviṣayā āsan tānadhi-
kṛtyādhikaraṇapraṇālyā vicārya nirṇayaṃ ca kṛtavantastadvidaḥ | yato hi vaidika-
vākyānyanusṛtya pracalati karmakāṇḍamataḥ vākyārthanirṇaya eva phalamasya śāstrasya |
etasmādeva hetorvākyaśāstratayāpyasyāḥ mīmāṃsāyāḥ vyapadeśaḥ | vākyānāṃ
tātparyāvadhāraṇaprakriyāṃ ca saṃkṣepeṇādhastānnirdiṣṭaṃ padyaṃ nirdiśati -
'upakramopasaṃhārāvabhyāso'pūrvatā phalam |
arthavādopapattī ca liṅga tātparyanirṇaye' || iti |
yājñikaiḥ dharmasūtrādīnāṃ sākṣādanugāmibhiḥ yajñasampādanaparamparāśritaiḥ
sākaṃ kadācid yajñayāgādiṣu bhavatyeva matadvaidhaṃ mīmāṃsakānāmiti śāstrasyā-
syādhyayanenāvagamyate |
tatra prathamādhyāye dharme ki pramāṇamiti jijñāsā samāhitā | vidhirathaṃ-
vāda: smṛtirnāma ceti caturvidhamiha pramāṇamiti caturṣu pādeṣu nirūpitam |
tathā hi vaidikaṃ vidhivākyaṃ pramāṇaṃ na veti saṃśaye vidhivākyaṃ na pramāṇaṃ

(8)
tadghaṭakasaṅgatyapekṣatvena nirapekṣatvābhāvāditi pūrvapakṣa: | padārthā evāvagatāḥ
vākyārthaṃ gamayanti, nāsti padārthebhyo'tirikto vākyārtho nāma | tataśca padavat
vākyasyāpyarthapratyāyane'styeva nirapekṣatvamiti nāstyaprāmāṇyaśaṅkāvasara iti
siddhāntaḥ |
prasaṅgato'traiva śabdasyārthasyobhayośca saṃbandhasya nityatvaṃ vedānāmapauruṣe-
yatvaṃ ca prasādhitam | anyathā puruṣagatabhramapramādādidoṣāṇāṃ tadīyakṛtiṣvapi
saṅkramaṇasambhavāttāsāṃ puruṣakṛtīnāmaprāmāṇyamāpadyeta |
jijñāsā bhavati yathā nyāyādidarśaneṣu pratyakṣādi pramāṇānāṃ vicāra
stathātrāpi teṣāṃ parīkṣā kathaṃ na kriyate | kevalaṃ vidhyādivākyānāmeva parīkṣā
kimarthamiti cet yathā pramāṇānāṃ pratyakṣādīnāṃ pramāṇatve'rthād yathārthajñānajana-
katve teṣāmavyabhicāritā heturasti nirdiṣṭā tathā vidhivākyānāṃ nāsti | paraiḥ
pratipakṣibhiḥ vedaprāmāṇyaniṣedhakaiḥ sphuṭamucyate codanārthād vaidikaṃ vidhivākyaṃ
vyabhicāravatī mithyāpratītijananīti, vidhivākyānāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ sādhayituṃ
prayatnasya āvaśyakatvāducitatvācceti | vivādasya nirṇayāyaiva mukhyato'na
śāstrārambhaḥ, siddhasya ca na jāyate sādhanamanāvaśyakatvāt | tasmādiha pratyakṣā-
dīnāṃ na parīkṣā |
evaṃ vidhyatiriktāni vaidikānyarthaṃ vādavākyānyapi bhavanti pramāṇāni |
yadyapi sameṣāmarthavādavākyānāṃ saṃgraho durghaṭastathāpi sthālīpulākanyāyenaika-
marthavādavākyamiha pramāṇatayā prasādhyate | vāyuvai kṣepiṣṭhā devateti vicārasya
viṣayaḥ | vākyasyāsya dharme prāmāṇyaṃ vartate na veti saṃśaye kartavyavidhāyaka
vākyānāmevānuṣṭheyarūpadharmaṃ prati prāmāṇyaṃ sambhavatīti nārthavādavākyānāṃ
prāmāṇyaṃ teṣāṃ siddhārthavarṇanamātraparatvāditi pūrvapakṣa: |
,
.
uttaraṃ tu yadyapyetādṛśavākyeṣu nāsti kiñcidanuṣṭhānavidhānaṃ tathāpi
tatprakaraṇasthaṃ yad vākyāntaramupalabhyate vāyavya śvetamālabheteti | tatra vartata
evānuṣṭheyasya chāgālambhanasya vidhānam | ta kavākyatayā ca 'vāyurve kṣepiṣṭhā
devate 'ti vākyaṃ netuṃ śakyam | tathā caikavākyatāpannayorvākyayorityamarthaḥ paryava-
syati - yasmād vāyuḥ kṣepiṣṭhā devatā tasmāttadarthaṃ śvetālambhanaṃ kartavyaṃ
kṣipralābhāyeti | evaṃ ca prāśastyavarṇanena puruṣapravartanaṃ prati vidhivākyasya

( 5 )
dharmāvabodhakaṃ prati bhavatyeva pramāṇamiti
evamaṣṭakā kartavyetyevaṃvidhāni vākyāni śrūyante | atha ca vaidiko
vidhirevāyamartho'nuṣṭheya iti nopalabhyate | smṛtistu nirdiśatyevamata eva
sandehaḥ—smṛtiḥ pramāṇaṃ na veti | smṛtirapramāṇaṃ dharmasya codanāmātralakṣaṇa-
tvāt | vedātiriktānāṃ smṛtīnāṃ prāmāṇyānarhatvāditi pūrvapakṣe siddhānta:
kriyate smṛtīnāṃ vedamūlatvena prāmāṇyameva | vaidikaiḥ smṛtā evārthāḥ smṛtiṣupa-
nibaddhāḥ na tatra nūtanaṃ kiñcidapi vidyate | ye ye'rthāḥ vedeṣu viprakīrṇā uktāste
sarve'tra puruṣopakārāya vedavicakṣaṇaiḥ smṛtikāraiḥ smṛtiṣu saṃgṛhītā iti tāsāṃ
siddhayatyeva prāmāṇyam | tasmāt smṛtayaḥ dharme pramāṇaṃ bhavitumarhanti |
udbhidā yajeta paśukāmaḥ' vākye'smin udbhicchabdo vicārasya
viṣayaḥ | atra saṃśayaḥ kimudbhidyate'neneti vyutpattyā tacchabdaḥ khanitrādi guṇaṃ
vidhatte atha vā tannāmakaṃ yāgaviśeṣamiti | kiñcid guṇaviśeṣa eva vidhīyate
khanitrādirūpaḥ | yathā dadhnā juhototi vākye dadhiśabdena dadhiguṇo vidhīyata
iti pūrvapakṣa: |
siddhāntastu dadhyādipadavadudbhidādiśabdasya prasiddhārthatvābhāvāt guṇa-
vidhitvaprayojakamatvarthaṃlakṣaṇāyā abhāvāt na yujyate'sya guṇavidhitvam |
tasmādasya śabdasya yāgaviśeṣanāmadheyataiva yuktā | phalata udbhinnāmakena
yāgena yajeteti vākyārthaḥ paryavasyati | tasmāt suṣṭhuktam-
.
vidhyarthavādasmṛtayaḥ nāma ceti caturvidham |
prathamādhyāyaḥ pādaiścatubhirmānamīritam || iti |
dharmaḥ kathaṃlakṣaṇa iti prathame'dhyāye vicāritam | ko dharmaḥ kāni
karmāṇi dharmavyapadeśabhāñjīti vicāro'dhunā prastūyate | ataḥ karmabhedo
mukhyatayā dvitīyādhyāyaviṣayaḥ | tadupodghātatadapavādatatprasaktānuprasaktatvenā-
nyo'rtho'tra gauṇaḥ | karmaṇāṃ bhedanirūpaṇāvasare'tra phalameva mukhyaṃ nirūpaṇīya-
miti kṛtvā tatsvarūpatattramāṇādivicāra evādāvupodghātarūpeṇa kriyate |
atra sandeho bhavati vidhivākye ki sarvaireva padairapūrvaṃ sambadhyate utaikenaiveti |

sarvareva padairapūrvaṃ sambadhyate tasya phalatvāt | sarveṣāṃ ca padānāṃ phalānva-
yitvāditi pūrvapakṣe ekenaiva padenāpūrvasya sambandhaḥ | ekasyānekaiḥ saha sambandha-
kalpane gauravāt | tasmādekamapūrvamekenaiva padena pratipādyate padantiraṃ ca taccheṣa-
tayānvetīti siddhāntaḥ |
idānīmaparā jijñāsā samudeti | ekenaiva padena pūrvaṃ pratipādyata iti
sādhite kiṃ tatpadaṃ yena tatpratipādyate | vidhivākyamevātra viṣayo bhavitumarhati |
tatra kiṃ vidhivākyagatanā mapadena pūrvamabhidhīyate utākhyātapadeneti saṃśaye
nāmapadenaivocyate'pūrvamiti pūrvapakṣa: | yato hi dravyādīnāṃ siddharūpāṇāmeva phalaṃ
prati sādhanatvasaṃbhavaḥ | siddhāntastu kriyāṃ vinā dravyāṇi phalaṃ sādhayituṃ na
kṣamante ataḥ ākhyātenaiva bhāvanāvācināpūrvaṃ pratipādyate na tu nāma
padeneti |
apūrvamākhyātapratyayabodhitenārthena
pratyāyyata ityuktam | apūrvaṃ
.
kimarthamaṅgīkaraṇīyamiti punarjajñāsā jāgartyeva | apūrvamasti na veti saṃśaye
niṣedhakoṭi : pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ | tatsadbhāve mānābhāvāt, svargādereva phalatvenā-
bhidhānāt tathā pratīyamānatvācceti tadyuktiḥ | siddhāntinastu vidhikoṭiḥ |
tathā hi jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajetetyatra yāgasya svargasādhanatvamuktam |
yathā caitat svargasādhanatvaṃ yāgasyopapadyeta tathāvaśyaṃ kalpanīyam | yāgānuṣṭhāna-
samāptyanantarameva svargo na siddhyatīti tatsamāptisvargaprāptyantarāle
ubhayānusyūtamubhayasambandhakaṃ kimapi kalpanīyam | anyathā ciravinaṣṭasya
yāgasya maraṇānantarabhāvinaṃ svargaṃ prati kāraṇatvaṃ na syāt, bahuvyāpāravyavahi-
tatvāt | tasmād vidhivākyoktayāgasvargayoḥ sādhyasādhakabhāvasāmañjasyāya
yatkimapi kalpyate tasyaiva vidhivyatiriktapramāṇāntarāgamyatvādapūrvamiti
nāma kṛtam | ata evābhihitaṃ bhagavatā śaṅkarācāryeṇa -- ' na cānutpādya
kimapyapūrvaṃ karma vinaśyat kālāntaritaṃ phalaṃ dātuṃ śaknoti | ataḥ karmaṇo
vā sūkṣmā kāciduttarāvasthā phalasya vā pūrvāvasthamapūrvaṃ nāmāstīti
tayate |'

( 7 )
tam | prasaṅgasaṅgatyā mantrabrāhmaṇādilakṣaṇamūhasvarūpamṛgādilakṣaṇaṃ cātraiva
pratipāditāni | dvitīyapādasya mukhyo viṣayaḥ karmabhedo nirūpyate |
ayamabhiprāyaḥ---ākhyātapratyayenaivāpūrvamucyata iti prasādhitam | tathā
caikaprakaraṇagatāni paryāyadhātu niṣpannānyākhyātāni yajati dadāti juhotītyā-
dīnyudāharaṇāni samupalabhyante | ataḥ sandeho bhavati kiṃ sarvairākhyātaiḥ bhinnaṃ
bhinnaṃ karmocyate utaikamiti | yasmāt sarveṣveka evākhyātapratyayo bhāvanāvācī
tasmādekaiva bhāvanā ekameva pūrvamekameva karma sarverucyate | na ca dhātubhedād-
bhedaḥ dhātorbhāvanāvācitvābhāvena tatrāprayojakatvāditi pūrvapakṣa:
siddhāntastu yadyapyākhyātameva bhāvanāyāḥ prayojakam | tathāpyākhyātasya
prati dhātubhinnatvameva tataśca bahūnāmākhyātānāmekaikadhātuviśeṣānuraktatvenaiva
bhāvanāvācitvena yāgadāna homabhāvanāḥ parasparaṃ bhinnā eveti | atra ca
śabdāntarameva karmabhede hetuḥ | ṣaḍvidhaḥ karmabhedaḥ pratipādito'sti | ata eva
karmabhedabodhakāni pramāṇāni ṣaṭ sañjāyante, yatraikaṃ śabdāntaramihodāhṛtam |
evamabhyāsaḥ, saṃkhyā, saṃjñā, guṇaḥ, prakriyā ca karmabhedaṃ bodhayanti |
abhyāsena karmabhedo yathā samidho yajati tanūnapātaṃ yajati iṃḍo yajati
bahiryajatīti vākyamiha yajaticatuṣṭayena kimekameva karmocyate uta catvārīti
saṃśaye, ekameva karmocyata iti pūrvapakṣaḥ, catvāri karmāṇi yajaticatuṣṭayaprayo-
gāditi siddhāntaḥ | karmaikye abhyāsasya nirarthakatāpatteḥ |
saṃkhyayā karmabhedo yathā 'saptadaśa prājāpatyān paśūnālabhete' ti viṣaya-
vākyam | atra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ vihitaṃ karmaikamevota saptadaśa karmāṇīti | ālabhe-
teti karmābhidhāyakasyaikatvādekamevātra karma saptadaśapaśvālambhanātmakamiti
pūrvapakṣe siddhāntastu prajāpatidevatākaṃ paśvālambhanarūpaṃ na karmaikam | tādṛśasya
karmaṇo bahutvāya bahuvacanaprayogastasmiṃśca bahutve viśeṣaḥ saptadaśasaṃkhyā
nirdiśyate | ato'tra saptadaśaiva karmāṇi karmabhedaśca saṃkhṣākṛta eva |
nāmadheyakṛtakarmabhedo yathā 'athaiṣa jyoti: athaiṣa viśvajyotiḥ athaiṣa
sarvajyotiḥ anena sahasradakṣiṇena yajeteti viṣayavākyam | atra saṃśayaḥ

(5)
kimekamevānyatra vihitaṃ karma jyotiṣṭomaṃ jyotiḥ śabdenānūdya tatra sahasra-
dakṣiṇatvarūpo guṇo vidhīyate āhostrit jyotirnāmakaṃ bhinnameva karma
vidhīyata iti | ekameva karma jyotiṣṭomamanūdya tatra sahasradakṣiṇatvarūpo guṇo
vidhīyata iti pūrvapakṣe athetyanena jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇasya vicchinnatvānnāstyatra
tena saṃbandha: | jyotiḥ śabdaścāpūrvameva nāmānyatrāvihitameva karmāntaraṃ
samarpayati | ato'tra jyotiṣṭomād bhinnānyeva jyoti: viśvajyotiḥ sarva-
jyotiriti tisṛbhiḥ saṃjñābhistrīṇi karmāṇi vidhīyanta iti siddhāntaḥ |
guṇakṛtakarmabhedo yathā ' tapte payasi dadhyānayati sā vaiśvadevyāmikṣā
vājibhyo vājinamiti' viṣayavākyam | tapte dugdhe dadhini prakṣipte yo ghanī
bhūtaḥ piṇḍo jāyate tasyaiva nāmāmikṣeti, avaśiṣṭajala bhāgasya ca vājinamiti |
tadetadatra vājibhyo vājinamityanena dvitīyena vākyena kiṃ karmāntaraṃ vidhīyate
uta pūrvavākyavihitaṃ karma pratyeva kiñcidguṇāntaraṃ vidhīyata iti saṃśayaḥ |
pūrvavākyena viśvebhyo devebhya āmikṣādravyasyārpaṇaṃ vihitaṃ dvitīyena ca vākyena
vaiśvadevadevatākaṃ yāgamanūdya vājinarūpadravyāntaraṃ vidhīyate āmikṣayā saha
samuccayārthaṃ vikalpārthaṃ veti pūrvapakṣe vaiśvadevayāgasya saha śiṣṭena | mikṣā dravyeṇā-
varuddhatvād dravyāntarasya tatra praveśāsaṃbhavāt vājinarūpadravyasampādanaka-
devatāntarameva 'vājibhya iti padena samarpyate | tataśca vājirūpadevatābhyo
yasmin karmaṇi vājinarūpadravyaṃ pradīyate tatkarma bhinnameva dvitīyena vākye -
nodhyate |
prakaraṇena karmabhedo yathā trivṛdagniṣṭudagniṣṭoma iti vākyasya sannidhā-
vidamaparaṃ vākyam – 'etasyaiva revatīṣu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma kṛtvā
paśukāmo hya etena yajete 'ti vākyamiha viṣayaḥ | atra dvitīyena vākyena ki
prakṛte'gniṣṭudyāga eva paśuphalāptaye revatīnāmṛcāṃ vāravantīyasāmnā saṃbandha-
karaṇaṃ vidhīyate uta tatsāmaguṇakaṃ paśuphalakamanyadeva karma vidhīyata iti
saṃśayaḥ | yathāgniṣṭuti brahmavarcasakāmasya vāyavyāsvṛkṣu agniṣṭomasāmagānaṃ
vihitaṃ vākyāntareṇa tathaivānenāpi vākyena tasminnevāgniṣṭudyāge revatīṣvṛkṣu
bāravantoyasāmagānatvaṃ vidhīyate paśukāmasya pūrvādhikaraṇīya nyāyeneti pūrvapakṣe
samādhānam tadyathātra hi dadhno homasādhanatvaṃ pratyakṣeṇa jñeyaṃ na śāstrakabodhyam |

ntaramevātra vidhīyata iti nirṇayaḥ | prasaṅgasaṅgatyātraṃva ṛgyajuḥ sāmnāṃ

tṛtīyapāde punaḥ karmaviśeṣāṇāṃ bhedastasyāpavādaśca nirūpito'sti |
tathā hi jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajeteti jyotiṣṭomavidhiḥ | tameva
jyotiṣṭomaṃ prakṛtya śrūyate yadi rathantarasāmā somaḥ syāt aindravāyavāgrān
grahān gṛhṇīyāt, yadi vṛhatsāmā śukrāgrān, yadi jagatsāmā āgrayaṇāgrāniti |
atra somaśabdena somalatāsādhanako yāgo vidhīyate | tasmiśca yāge mādhya-
ndine savane pṛṣṭhastotre rathantarabṛhajjagannāmakāni sāmāni vikalpena
vihitāni | aindravāyavaḥ, maitrāvaruṇaḥ, āśvinaḥ, śukraḥ, manthyāgrayaṇaḥ, ukthyaḥ,
dhruva ityādi nāmakā somarasadhārakā pātraviśeṣā grahanāmadheyā bhavanti |
rathantarasāmopetatve somayāge aindravāyavaḥ, vṛhatsamopetatve śukraḥ jagatsāmopetatve
āgrayaṇa ityetad viṣayavākyārthaḥ |
atra saṃśayo bhavati iha yo grahāgratā viśeṣo vidhīyate sa kiṃ jyotiṣṭo-
masyaiva rathantarasāmādiyuktasyātha vā rathantarasāmādināmnaḥ karmāntarasyeti |
prakṛto jyotiṣṭomaḥ gāyatrādisāmopetastasyaiva vyāvṛttyarthamiha vākye
rathantarādayo guṇāḥ kīrtyante | ata aindravāyavādigrahāgratvaviśiṣṭāni
karmāntarāṇyevātra vidhīyanta iti pūrvapakṣa: |
siddhāntastu prakaraṇādatra jyotiṣṭomasyaiva grahaṇam | gṛhīte ca tasmin
tadviṣaye grahāgratvaviśeṣo guṇo vidhīyate na ca rathantarādyabhidhānena jyotiṣṭomasya
vyāvṛttiḥ | prātaḥsavane tasya gāyatrādisāmopetatve'pi pṛṣṭhastotre rathantarā-
diyogasyāpi saṃbhavāditi | atrāpavādā api nirdiṣṭā bahavastasmādapavāda-
vacanameva pādārthaḥ| tathā coktam-
upodghātaḥ karmabhedamānaṃ tasyāpavādagīḥ |
prayogabheda ityete dvitīyādhyāyapādagāḥ || iti |

|
idānīmavaśiṣṭasya caturthapādaviṣayasya prayogabhedasya svarūpanirūpaṇam |
paryavasite karmabhedanirūpaṇe tatsaṃbaddha eva prayogabhedo vicāryate | 'yāvajjīva-
magnihotraṃ juhotī 'ti viṣayavākyam | atra saṃśayaḥ - kirmatra yāvajjīvikatā
karmadharma āhostrit kartṛdharmaḥ | 'agnihotraṃ juhuyātsvargakāma' ityanena
yatkāmyamagnihotraṃ vihitaṃ tadeva juhotītyanenānūdya yāvajjīvapadena tasya
maraṇāvadhikālo vidhīyate | tathā cedaṃ vākyaṃ kāmyamagnihotramuddiśya
yāvajjīvakālaṃ vidadhāti | evaṃ cāsya kāmyānuṣṭhānasya maraṇaparyantatā
siddhyati | anyathā kāmyāgnihotrakarmaṇoḥ yāvajjīvamabhyāsa siddhyarthameva
yāvajjīvakālarūpasya karmadharmasyaivāyaṃ vidhiḥ | tathā ca nityakāmyaprayogoṃ
sammilitāvekameva karma yāvajjīvavyāpyaṃ sampādayata iti pūrvapakṣe yāvajjīva-
padasya satyapi kālalakṣakatve na kālavācakatvam | kṛtsnajīvanaparyāya evedaṃ
padam na ca jīvanaṃ kasyacit karmaṇo dharmo bhavitumarhati tasya puruṣadharmatvāt |
tameva puruṣadharmaṃ nimittīkṛtyāgnihotraprayogo'tra vākye vidhīyate | na cātra karma-
bhedaḥ prayogabheda evātra labhyate | na ca prayoganairantaryāpattiḥ sāyaṃ prātaḥkāla-
yoreva spaṣṭaṃ vihitatvāt | tasmād jīvanasya puruṣadharmatvāt nitya kāmyaprayogī
bhinnāveveti siddhāntaḥ |
evamuktān ṣaṭ karmabhedakān vihāyānyaḥ karmabhedaka: śākhābhedādirūpo-
'sti na veti vicāryate |
samupalabhyante darśapūrṇamāsavidhāyakāni vākyāni nānāśākhāsu kāṭha-
kāṇva mādhyandinataittirīyādyāsu | tatra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ pratiśākhaṃ bhinnameva karma
darśapūrṇamāsākhyaṃ vidhīyate utaikameveti | kāṭhakādayo nāmabhedāste ca karma-
bhedakā iti śākhābhedena karma bhinna miti pūrvapakṣe sarvatroktasya darśapūrṇa māsasyaika-
meva rūpam | darśapūrṇamāseti nāmāpyekameva sarvatropalabhyate | tasmādananyathā siddha-
rūpapratyabhijñānāt śākhābhedo na bhavati karmabhedaka iti siddhāntaḥ |
tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ śarabhapādātmakaḥ śeṣādhyāyanāmnā prasiddhaḥ | śeṣapada-
mihāṅgavācakam | pradhānāṅgabhāvo hi karmasu svābhāvikaḥ | nahi sarvaṃ karma
pradhānameva sañjāyate na vāṅgameva | ekatraiva yāgayajñādiṣu pradhānāṅgabhāvaḥ
karmasūpalabhyate | tasmāt karmaṇāmaṅgapradhānabodhakāni sarvāṇi vākyānyatra

vicāraḥ |
0
aṅgasya [śeṣasya ] lakṣaṇaṃ sambhavati naveti saṃśaye niṣedhakoṭi:
pūrvapakṣiṇām | tathā hi aṅgatvaṃ [śeṣatvaṃ ] nāma kimavinābhūtatvaṃ prayojyatvaṃ,
vidhyantavihitatvaṃ vā ? nāva:, tathā sati svāmyapi bhṛtyasya śeṣaḥ syāt |
na dvitīyaḥ, puroḍāśakapālena tuṣānupanapatītyatra tuṣopavāpaṃ prati śeṣasyāpi
kapālasya tatprayojyatvābhāvād śeṣatvaṃ na syāt | nāpi tṛtīyaḥ, vidhyādi
vihitasya palāśaśākhācchedasya satyapi śeṣatve vidhyantavihitatvābhāvāt |
tasmānnāsti śeṣasya lakṣaṇam | na vā tatprasādhakaḥ kaściddheturasti | vimataḥ
śeṣaḥ evaṃ rūpatvāditi kasyacidhetornirūpayitumaśakyatvāt |
.
atra siddhāntaḥ, vimataḥ prayājādiḥ śeṣaḥ parārthatvāt bhṛtyādivaditi
hetu sunirūpaḥ | tathā ca parārthaḥ śeṣa iti lakṣaṇamadṛṣṭameva | tasmāt śeṣatvasya
hetuḥ svarūpaṃ ca nirūpitaṃ bhavati | phalataḥ kaḥ śeṣaḥ kathaṃ śeṣaḥ kena hetunā śeṣa
iti jijñāsātra nirasyate | śeṣaḥ parārthatvāditi jaiminimūtraṃ parasya pradhāna-
karmaṇa upakārakamaṅgaṃ śeṣapadenābhidadhātītyatra nirvivādam |
adhunā śeṣasya lakṣaṇe nirūpite tasya lakṣyaṃ nirdiśyate | tathā hi
svacakapālādikaṃ dravyam, aruṇimādiko guṇaḥ, avaghātaprokṣaṇādikaḥ saṃskāraḥ
darśapūrṇamāsādikaṃ karma svargādiphalaṃ kartā puruṣaśceti bhavanti yajñāṅgāni | yatra
śrutivākye etānyuktāni tāni sarvāṇyatra viṣayavākyatvena parigṛhyante | atra
saṃśayo bhavati kiṃ dravyādayaḥ puruṣāntāḥ sarva eva śeṣasya lakṣyā uta kānicide-
veti | dravyaguṇasaṃskārā eva śeṣāḥ na karmaphalapuruṣāḥ | dravyādiṣu triṣveva
niyataḥ śeṣabhāvaḥ iti pūrvapakṣa: | siddhāntastu yathā dravyādayastrayaḥ parārthāstathaiva
dharmaphalapuruṣā api | tasmānnirdiṣṭānāṃ sameṣāṃ śeṣatvam | tathā hi darśapūrṇa-
māsādi phalārthameva sampadyate puruṣaścodumbarī sammānādiṣu guṇabhūtaḥ śrūyate |
iyān viveko'tra bhavati | dravyaguṇasaṃskārāṇāṃ yāgaṃ prati śeṣatvaṃ niyataṃ
karmādīnāṃ tvāpekṣikam | karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ prati śeṣatve'pi dravyaṃ prati pradhānatvameva |
phalasya puruṣaṃ prati śeṣatve'pi yāgaṃ prati prādhānyameva, puruṣasyodumbarī sammānādi

( 12 )
prati śeṣatve'pi phalaṃ prati prādhānyamiti | īdṛśasyāṅgatvasya vicāraḥ prathama-
pādaviṣayaḥ |
O
śrutiliṅgavākyaprakaraṇasthānasamākhyānāṃ pāradaurbalyamarthavipratiṣedhā-
diti sūtreṇa nirdiṣṭeṣu śrutiprāmāṇyamāsādya śeṣatvavicāra: prathame pāde
vihitaḥ | liṅgapramāṇenāṅgatvavicāra iha dvitīye pāde vidhīyate | liṅgamiha
śabdasāmarthyam | tathā coktam — sāmarthyaṃ sarvaśabdānāṃ liṅgamitya bhidhīyate'
iti | tasmādraḍhāvasya tātparyam | samākhyā tu yogikīti tasyāḥ bhedo'va-
dhāraṇīyaḥ | bahirdevasadanaṃ dāmīti bahirlavanaprakāśako mantrastatprakāśana-
liṅgena lavanakriyāyāṃ viniyujyate lavitavyaṃ ca bahidvividhaṃ bhavati mukhyaṃ
goṇañceti | mukhyaṃ kuśakāśādi daśavidhadarbharūpam gauṇaṃ tu tatsadṛśaṃ
tṛṇāntaram | atra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ mukhyasyaiva kuśāderlavane mantrasya viniyoga
uta gauṇasyāpi tṛṇāntarasyeti pūrvapakṣe'tra mukhyavad gauṇasyāpi śabdenocya
mānatvaṃ tulyam | tathā ca mukhyavad gauṇe'pi barhiṣi lavanaṃ prāptaṃ tatra lavana
prakāśakamantrasya viniyogo bhavitumarhati | siddhāntastu mukhyasya śīghratara-
pratītatvena tasmin mantraṃ viniyujya caritārthaṃ lavanaprakāśanasāmarthyarūpaṃ
liṅgaṃ vilambita pratītatayā gauṇaṃ pratīkṣate | tasmān mukhyasyaiva barhiṣo lavane
mantro viniyojanīya iti | evamanyeṣāmapyadhikaraṇānāṃ vicāra iti sthālī-
pulākanyāyenātra viṣayasya digdarśanaṃ kṛtam | phalataḥ liṅgasya svarūpaṃ teṣu
prābalyaṃ daurbalyaṃ ca vicāritam |
vākyamūlakatvasyāṅgatvasya vicārastṛtīyapāde'tra vihitaḥ | tathā hi
uccairṛ cā kriyate upāṃśu yajuṣā uccaiḥ sāmneti jyotiṣṭome śrūyate | atra
sandeho bhavati uccaistvādayo dharmā mantrāṇāmuta vedānāmiti | mantradharmā
uccaistvādaya iti pūrvapakṣa: | vedadharmāste iti siddhāntaḥ | yato hi vidhi-
vākyānāmupakrame śrūyate trayo vedā asṛjyanta agneḥ ṛgvedaḥ vāyoryajurvedaḥ
ādityātsāmaveda iti | etadupakramopasaṃhāraikavākyatābalenopasaṃhārabhūteṣu
vidhivākyeṣvapi ṛgyajuḥ sāmaśabdāḥ vedaparā eva neyāḥ | atraiva prakaraṇasya
sthānakramasya samākhyāyāśca yathākramaṃ prāmāṇyamāsādyāṅgatvavicāraḥ vihitaḥ |
punaretāsāṃ samākhyādīnāṃ parasparaṃ virodhe kā gatirityapi vicāritam |

( 13 )
caturthapādasyāpi vicāraviṣayaḥ śrutiprakaraṇayoḥ liṅgavākyayośca

vādatvavidhitvādinirṇayahetutvaṃ śrutyādīnāṃ ca parasparavirodhasadasadbhāva viveka-
ścātra vidyate |
pacamapādasya viṣayaḥ virodhapratipattī kiṃ kāryamiti | tathā hi
darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ śeṣakāryāṇi śrūyante 'uttarārdhāt sviṣṭakṛte samavadyati'
'iḍāmupahvayatī'tyādi | tatra sandehaḥ kimupāṃśuyājadravyāt ājyāt sviṣṭa-
kṛdādi śeṣāvadānaṃ kartavyamuta neti | vidhipakṣo'tra pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ | sarvebhyo
havirbhyaḥ samavadyati iti vākyena sarvebhyo havirbhyaḥ śeṣakāryaṃ prāpitam niṣedha -
pakṣastu siddhāntinaḥ | sampanna prayojanasyaiva havirdravyasya śeṣaḥ śeṣāvadānarūpāṃ
pratipattimapekṣate | dhrauvaṃ tvājyaṃ na sampannaprayojanam | tena kartavyānāmupa-
staraṇādīnāmasampannatvāt | tasmādājyena na bhavati śeṣakāryamiti |

prakṛtivad vikṛtiḥ kartavyetyatideśena vikṛtāvapi prāpnoti | evaṃ ca prakṛtau
prāptāyā juhūparṇatāyā vikṛtāvatideśavākyenaiva prāptatve yadi tasyā eva punaḥ
sākṣāt vidhyantareṇa vidhānaṃ syāt tarhi dviruktireva syāt, sā tu na nyāyyā |
tasmādanārabhya vidhānavicāraḥ ṣaṣṭhapāde vihitaḥ |
saptame pāde bahupradhānopakārakādīnāṃ prayājādīnāṃ vicāraḥ pradarśitaḥ
paramaṅgatvamadhikṛtyaiva | ata eva vākyaprakaraṇābhyāmaṅgatvaniyamanamiha viṣayaḥ |
aṣṭame tu pāde svāmyartho nirūpitaḥ | tathā hi jyotiṣṭomādī dakṣiṇā
dānena karmakarā āsādayitavyāḥ | etadeva teṣāṃ parikraya ityucyate | atra
saṃśayaḥ idaṃ parikrayarūpaṃ karma kena kartavyam yajamānenāhosvidadhvaryuṇā anyena

evaṃ ca kratvarthapuruṣārthayorlakṣaṇe sthite kaḥ padārthaḥ kratvarthaḥ kaśca

vacanaṃ pradarśanārtham | evaṃ ca sati pratyakṣaśru tā ekatvādikalpā abādhitā-
stiṣṭhanti | tasmāt sarve kalpāḥ jyotiṣṭome vikalpitā iti siddhāntaḥ |
satravicārātmako'tra paṣṭhaḥ pādaḥ | saptadaśāvarāḥ satramāsīranniti
śrutivākyam | prayājeṣu cānuṣṭhānaprakāre puruṣabhedakṛtaḥ bhedo vidyate | kaṇva
kāśyapādigotrāṇāṃ nārāśaṃso dvitīyaḥ prayājaḥ, itareṣāṃ tu tanūnapādeva |
ta ete bhinnakalpāḥ | tatra saṃśayaḥ, satreṣu kiṃ samānakalpānāṃ bhinnakalpānāṃ ca
sahādhikāraḥ āhosvit samānakalpānāmeva sahādhikāra iti saptadaśāvarā
iti nirviśeṣaṃ sāmānyamevoktāḥ satriṇaḥ | na copalabhyate bhinnakalpānāṃ
sahānuṣṭhānaniṣedhaḥ | tasmāt samānakalpāḥ bhinnakalpāśca sarve'trādhikāriṇaḥ
satreṣviti pūrvapakṣe, siddhāntaḥ bhinnakalpānāṃ sahānuṣṭhāne karma viguṇaṃ syāt |
yasmin kāle kaṇvagotrīyasya nārāśaṃsaḥ prāpnoti tasminneva kāle'parasya
tanūnapāt prāptaḥ | paraspareṇa viparītakaraṇe karmavaiguṇyam, karmavaiguṇye ca satra
phalābhāvastasmāt samānakalpānāmeva sahādhikāra iti |
yajñe ki deyaṃ kiṃ ca na deyamiti vicāraḥ saptamapādaviṣayaḥ |
'viśvajiti sarvasvaṃ dadātī 'ti śrutivākyam | sarvasvadānamiha vihitam |
atra sandeho bhavati yāvat kiñcidiha svaśabdenābhipretam āhosvit yatra
yajamānasya prabhutvayogaḥ | kiṃ pitrādīnāmapi dānaṃ dhanādidānamiva kāryam

lakṣaṇāśrayaṇasya, vidhyāditattvanirṇītiḥ pramāṇenaiva sthitā | samasto hi prathamaḥ
pādaścodanāsūtra parikaraḥ | tatra ca dharmasya codanālakṣaṇatvamuktam | ato'va-
dhāritārthalakṣaṇaśabdasāhacaryāt pramāṇaśabdaḥ pratipādyata ityevaṃ vyutpādya dharmaviṣaya
eva vyākhyeyaḥ | satyapi prameyamātravācitve, asmin śāstre jijñāsyatvena dharmaḥ
prameya: prakrānta iti sa eva gṛhyate | atha vā karaṇameva pramāṇaṃ tasyaiva lakṣaṇa-
muktam | yadyapi ca sarvapramāṇānāṃ tannoktaṃ tathāpi dharmapramāṇasya codanātvaṃ
lakṣaṇamityetāvataivoktaṃ manyate | na cāvaśyaṃ sarvātmanaiva lakṣite lakṣaṇatvam |
lakṣaṇaṃ cātrānubhāṣitaṃ codanālakṣaṇābhidhānāśrayaṃ vidhyādi tattvanirṇotiśca
yātrānubhāṣitā sā prāmāṇyenaiva saṃsthitā | prathame'dhyāye tadatiriktena rūpeṇa
tasyāstatrāsthitatvāditi |

vācyā viklittyadhiśrayaṇādayaḥ karmasthāḥ kartṛsthāśca | tatra yadīdāsīnya-
pracyutimātreṇa parispandarūpaṃ nirūpyate sā bhāvanā | ye tu tadanurañjanasamarthā
kartṛ karmagatā viśeṣāste tasyā eva karaṇetikartavyatāṃśayoniviśante | evaṃ tahi
ta eva ca dhātvarthā iti | pacirapi na vikledamātravacanaḥ siddhayati
viklittibhyāmaviśeṣaprasaṅgāt | ato vikledanābhisandhikṛtamadhiśrayaṇā-
dyapyupādatte | atha vā viśeṣeṇaivamucyate - ta eva dhātvarthā iti | tatra cyajau
tenaiva dhātunocyate pacāvadhiśrayatyādinā | sarvathā dhātvarthā eva vyāpāra-
viśeṣāḥ prayojakavyāpāro na punaḥ karotyevaṃrūpo'paro vyāpāro'stīti |
dhātvarthavyatirekeṇeti | dhātvartha saṃsargo'syāḥ śabdato vastutaśca sarvadāvasthito
yadvaśenopajātabhramairanyonyarūpāveśenaika evādhiśrayaṇādiḥ sādhyasvabhāvo
vyāpāraviśeṣa ityucyate | śakyate caiṣā nipuṇadṛṣṭi bhivivektum | tathā hi
adhiśrayaṇaṃ karoti pārka karotīti bhidyamāneṣvapi pākādidhātvartheṣvabhidya-
māno kathaṃ dhātvartho'ntarbhavet |
-
tatra
evaṃ pratyayātirekādavagatātirekāyāḥ pratyayaviṣayaṃ nirūpayati
yadīdāsīnyapracyutimātreṇeti | prayatnaparispandayorudāsīnatva vicchedaḥ sāmā-
nyātmā karotyartha ityaṅgīkaraṇe viśeṣapradarśanamidaṃ parispandarūpamiti | yadā tu
sidhyatphalodayānukūlatāmātraṃ karoti phalamityetaj jñānavedyātyantamanyānapekṣā
dravyasya phalodaye vṛttiḥ kriyeṣyate tadā prayatnasya pratibandhavaśena phalānudaye-
Scyapracyutasvarūpasya saṃyogavyavadhinā ca phale hetostadapekṣasya na svarūpeṇa
kriyātvam | nahi kriyā ca syānna kriyate ca kiśviditi saṃbhavati, svabhāvā-
ntarābhāvāt | parispanda eva tu saṃyogavibhāgayoratyantamanyānapekṣaḥ kriyā |

pratiprayogamevaṃ vyavasthiteti | atha vā yasyotpattyā vyāpāraḥ paryavasyati |
tadvyāpārasyāpavargasampatsthā kārakaṃ karmetyucyate | dhātvarthāvasānaścākhyātā-
bhidhānādaravidhīyamāno vyāpāraḥ tadanantara bhāvyenābhidhānopāttena rūpeṇa
na karma gṛhṇātīti tasya prācuryam | prayogasyāvyavahitaphaloddeśena tu tadava-
sānatā vyāpārasya nābhidhānopāttā | vivakṣayā tvagniṃ cinute ityādau
sampādyā | vakṣyati hyetāvāneva vartamānaḥ kāla iti | tena tātparyābhidhā-
yina eva bhāvanākarmatvopapādakasyaivaṃ kāmaśabdasya tatra prayogaḥ pracura iti |
dhātvarthenānupasthāpite karmaṇyakarmake dhātau bhāvanā svamahimalabdhenaiva
karmaṇopapadyate ityuktopasthāpite'pi nāvaśyaṃ tadeva gṛhṇātīti yathāprastuta eva
yajatāvityāha-yajerapīti | athavoktamidaṃ kvāciddhātvarthakarmaiva |
bhāvanā
bhāvanā karma kvacidanyaditi | tatra yajetetyatra kathamityata āha-
yajerapi tviti | bhāvanā karmaṇaiva tadviśeṣasya dhātvarthasya sarvatraiva sakarma-
phatvotpatteḥ kathaṃ tadvibhāga iti pṛcchati - kastahīṃti |

siddhiḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ kramotpattīnāṃ pradhānāpūrvāṇāṃ kramavattibhirevāṅgāpūrvaiḥ prāgūrdhvaṃ

lābhāttannirapekṣaṃ tadānutpannapradhānāntarāpekṣaṃ ca prathamakṛtasāmavāyikāṅgāpūrva-
mātrānugṛhītaṃ svāpūrvameva tāvatsādhayati | evaṃ yadā darśāpūrvāṇi tadaṅgā
pūrvāṇyapi samastāni niṣpannāni tadā tadīyabrāhmaṇatarpaṇānantaramaparyāyavidhānād-
yāgeṣu tyāgaprakṣepayorhomeṣu 'dravye'nvaya iti | kathaṃ punaḥ kramotpattīnāmiti |

rapūrva jamyate tato'ṅgāpūrvāṇi sarveṣāṃ yugapadupakuryuḥ | kramavatāṃ tu prathama-

sāmastyenāvidyamānaiḥ kathaṃ yugapadupakatu śakyamiti | prakramavaśāditi | prakāratā-

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
bhrāntitvaṃ pratipadyate | sarvatraiva lakṣaṇāśrayaṇamānarthakyaprasaṅganimittam | anyathā
mukhyārthopapatteḥ |
tena virodhāt saktasādhanakahomavidhānaṃ prakaraṇasaṃbandhi gṛhyate | nanu saktū-
nāmapīti | homavantaḥ saktavo'pi jyotiṣṭomasyopakṛtyārthavanto bhaviṣyantī-
tyāha | siddhāntavādī tu cchalena homasādhanībhūtānāmevārthavattvamabhyupagacchati
ko vā brūte neti | sarvatraiva ca-
dravyāṇāṃ vākyasaṃyuktakkriyānirvartanādvinā |
na prayojanamastyanyat kathaṃbhāvādyasaṃgateḥ ||
yathā homa: kriyātmakatvāt kimityapekṣamāṇaḥ pradhānakathaṃbhāvenetthamanu-
grāhakatvena gṛhyate naivaṃ dravyam | na ca tasmādadṛṣṭakalpanā, kriyānirvatryatvāda-
pūrvāṇām |
yatrāpi tāvaddravyaṃ sākṣātphalāya codyate yathā godohanena paśukāma
iti, tatrāpyanyaprayojanakriyādvāramantareṇa nāpūrvaṃ bhaviṣyati | kimuta yadā na kasya-
cidaṅgatvena saktavaḥ śrūyante | na ca kiñcit kalpanāpramāṇamasti | dvitīyeti cet,
na hītyuttaram | dvitīyā hi saktūnāṃ prādhānyaṃ vadantī homaṃ tadarthaṃ kuryānna saktu-
bhyo'pūrvamiti pratipattim | tena dvitīyāprasādād bhaveddhomaḥ saktvarthaḥ | kathaṃ nāma
ca lakṣaṇādyāśrayaṇīyā 'na śaktikalpaneti | anyathā mukhyārthopapatteriti |
yataḥ ānarthakyaprasaṅgābhāve'pi mukhyārthagrahaṇamupapattimat atastannimittameva
lokalakṣaṇādyāśrayaṇaśabdasya | ata ihāpi tadeva śabdavṛttamucitamiti |
nanu saktūnāmapi prakaraṇapāṭhāt prayojanavatteti bhāṣyamanubhāvya vyācakṣāṇo
nirvyāpārebhyo'pūrva nivṛttimapaśyan vākyasamarpitayā hūyamānāvasthayopete-
myo'pūrva' nirvṛttimāha- homavanta iti |
kriyānirvartyatvāditi | pūrvāparakālayorvyabhicāriṇo dravyarūpānnā-
ntarāpūrvamutpattumarhati | yadyapyarhati tathāpi kathaṃ bhāvādyasambandhād dravyasya
nāstyatra prakaraṇamiti bhāṣyaṃ, tasyārthamāha na ca kiñciditi | nanu dvitīyā
vibhaktiriti pramāṇaśaṅkābhāṣyaṃ tasyārthamāha - dvitīyeti cediti |
atrottarabhāṣyaṃ, nahi dvitīyāvibhaktirityādi tadvibhajya vyācaṣṭe

[ se
homavyāptāḥ saktavaḥ syuriti | nānyasmai prayojanāyāśravaṇāt | te cāmī bhavanti
home kṛte saktavo homasaṃbaddhā iti prāgeva prayojanalābhāt paryavasitā dvitīyā
niṣprayojaneṣvapi, homaṃ prati prādhānyamātreṇa karmatvasiddheḥ | tatra siddhe vākyārthe
paścānna puruṣasya kratorvopayujyanta iti jñātvā tadvacanādvādṛṣṭaṃ kalpyeta | prādhānya
jñānaṃ vā bhrāntiriti | tatrāha-na ca tadvacanācchakyamanyat kalpayitumiti |
prādhānyāvivakṣaiva nyāyyā | tataśca tṛtīyārthasiddhiriti matvā mahābhāṣya
kāreṇoktaṃ tṛtīyāyā: sthāne dvitīyeti | tena vaiṣamyaṃ homasaṃmārgayoḥ, saktūnāṃ
niṣprayojanatvena tadarthatvāt puroḍāśādeścānyaśeṣabhāvena paryagnikaraṇādisaṃskārā-
rhatvāt| dṛḍhe ca śāstrārthe'vasthite dṛṣṭopakārābhāve'pyadṛṣṭakalpanā bhaviṣyati |
loke'pyadṛṣṭārthāḥ saṃskārā ācārād gamyante | yathā grāmāntarādāgatānāṃ śāntyartha
magnilavaṇadadhyakṣataprabhṛteḥ śirasa upari bhrāmaṇaṃ śucideśapratiṣṭhāpanaṃ ca |
na cāsyānupapattirācāraprāmāṇyāt | tenādṛṣṭārthānāmapi tulyaśrutitvāt
siddho guṇabhāvaḥ | nanvevaṃ sati sarvatra prayojanavattā kṛtameva dravyaprādhānyamiti
na vaktavyaṃ tulyaśrutitvāt iti darśayitavye vā yatra prayojanavattā nirapekṣaṃ dvitīyā-
kṛtameva prādhānyaṃ kevala tṛtīya| kṛto vā guṇabhāva iti | jñaducyate, prādhānyaṃ tāvat
'aindrayā gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhata' ityatra liṅgādindrārthatvāvagate garhipatyeka vākya-
tvācca tatsādhanakendropasthānaprasaṅge kevaladvitīyākṛtameva gārhapatyaprādhānyam |
tathā sūktavākena prastaramityubhayoḥ prayojanavattvāviśeṣe kevala tṛtīyā-
kṛtameva sūktavākasya prastarapraharaṇāṅgatvam | tena vibhaktibhirevāṅgāṅgibhāve-
nāma homavyāptāḥ saktavaḥ syurityuddeśāddhomānuṣṭhāne homaḥ saktvarthaḥ
syāditi | nānyasmai prayojanāyeti | dvitīyāprasādānnānyasmai prayojanāya
saktavo bhaveyurasyārthasya dvitīyāto'śravaṇāditi | tatra saktvarthatevāmumartha-
mantarbhāvayatītyāha- te cāmī iti | tatrāha-na ca tadvacanāditi |
homavidhistasya prayojanamākāṃkṣati | saktvarthatayā prayojanamanāsādayan pari-
hṛtya saktvarthatāṃ prayojanāntaraṃ homādeva mṛgayate | yathākāṃkṣe ca kalpanātī
homādeveti | ācāraprāmāṇyāditi | yādṛśena śabdenādṛṣṭārthasaṃskārapratipādanaṃ
śakyaṃ sa eva tatra kalpanīya ityabhiprāyaḥ |

'yamityavasīyate |

anutkṛṣyamāṇa eva vā pragāthaḥ sannidhivaśādavayavadvāreṇa mahendramavirodhāya
dṛṣṭārthatvāya ca prakāśayiṣyati, yathāgnīṣomīye manotāyāmagniśabdaḥ prakaraṇāt
samāsasthaḥ pratīyamānaḥ sāhacaryātsomamapi pratipādayan samudāyārtho vijñāyate |
ataścānutkarṣe'pyadoṣa iti || 21 ||
āmnāteṣvapi mantreṣu notkṛṣṭeṣvarthadarśanam |
sarveṣu bhavatītyevaṃ notkṛṣṭatvaṃ tu duṣyati ||
na hi yamādīnāṃ grahāntareṣvapi devatātvaṃ yenendravadutkṛṣṭairmantraḥ prakā-
śyeran | atasteṣāṃ tāvad vidhyānarthakyaprasaṅgādavaśyamadṛṣṭārthatvena bhavitavyam |
tataśca tatsāmanyāditareṣu tathātvaṃ, tasmānna saṃskārakarmatvamiti ||22||
yamādayo na vidyante yadyapi grahadevatāḥ |
anyakarmopayogitvātprakāśyāḥ syustathāpi naḥ ||
maṇḍūkasūktasyāgnau prayogaḥ, tatra maṇḍūkenāgnivikarṣaṇāmnānāt akṣa-
sūktasya rājasūye, tatra 'akṣaiddivyatīti' vidhānāt |

prasaktānuprasakteti patitamṛgādilakṣaṇābhidhānam | śabdārthasya vṛddhavyavahāra -

ajitopetam
[ 10ε
yāvatā padasaṃvena smāyaṃte yāgasādhanam |
tāvadyajuśca vākyaṃ ca vaktavyatvānnirūpyate ||
vacanārhaṃ hi vākyaṃ kārye samartha ca tadahaṃ bhavati na nyūnamadhikaṃ vā,
tāvataiva cejyate | tattu kīdṛśamitye kārya sākāṅkṣāvayavaṃ ceti lakṣaṇam |
īdṛśaṃ cedekaṃ yajuryattato'dhikaṃ tad vākyāntaraṃ yajurantaraṃ cetyuktaṃ bhavati |
I
evaṃ sarvatra purastāt parastād vaikārthatvasamarthapadaparigrahaṇādasamartha padavimo-
kena ca bhinnāni yajūṃṣi kalpyante | tenaikavākya lakṣaṇoktī cārthād yajurbhedavākya-
bhedalakṣaṇayoḥ siddhiriti etasmāccet kāraṇādityāha | kimudāharaṇamiti |
abhidheyavācinamarthaśabdaṃ gṛhītvā lakṣaṇadvayasamāveśā saṃbhāvanayā pṛcchati |
tathā hi---
ekārthatvaṃ padasya syāt sākāṅkṣāvayavaṃ na tat |
sākāṅkṣāvayavaḥ saṃgharatasyaiko'rtho na vidyate ||
,
padasya hyākṛtirnāmaiko'rtho bhavenna tu tadavayavānāṃ varṇānāṃ prakṛtipratyaya-
yorvā vibhāge sākāṅkṣatvam, arthapratyāyanaśaktyabhāvāt samūhāvayavāstu
padāni vibhāge parasparamākāṅkṣanti, na tveṣāmekārthatvaṃ, pratipadamarthabhedāt :
samūhasya ca pṛthagarthāntarāprasiddha: | ato lakṣyābhāvādalakṣaṇametaditi |
prayojanavācyabhiprāyeṇa
paraḥ
devasya tvetyudāhṛtam | nanviti
svābhiprāyaṃ prakaṭayati | yadā padārthavyatiriktaḥ kaścideko nirbhāgo vākyārtho nāsti
tadā tattatpadārthadharmāveva sāmīpyavaśātteṣāṃ gamyamānī bhedasaṃsargau syātām |
ata āha— tathāpyekārthaṃtā na syāt | katham --
bhedo nāma padārthānāṃ vyavacchedaḥ parasparam |
sa ca pratipadārthatvānnaikatvaṃ pratipadyate ||
anena ca yajurbhedalakṣaṇopekṣitaṃ tadbhedapratipādanameva kriyata ityāha-
īdṛśaṃ ceti | sūtrakārīyāllakṣaṇādabhimatasiddheḥ pratipādakaṃ bhāṣyame-
tasmāccet kāraṇādityādi | abhimataṃ ca sākṣād yajurbhedajñānam anu-
ṣaṅgācca vākyamātralakṣaṇam | tenobhayasiddhipratipādanena bhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe-
tenaikavākyalakṣaṇokta veti | yadetarapadārthoparaktaṃka padārthaparāṇi sarvāṇi padāni
tadā sa eva tāvadeko'rthaḥ sarveṣāṃ bhavati |

tasmātkarma samavāyitvena dṛṣṭārthatvādaśeṣasavitrādipadārthaviśiṣṭanirvāpaprakāśanaṃ
samastasya padasamuhasyaikaṃ prayojanamiti siddhamudāharaṇam | parastu nirvāpa-
prādhānyamajñātvā pṛcchati - nanvatra devasyeti | siddhāntavādyāha-yadi nirvapāmī-
tyanuṣaṅgastata evaṃ sa cānuṣaṅgastadguṇabhāvena bhavet, na cāsya guṇabhāva:
prayojanavattvāt | savitrādīnāṃ cākarmasamavāyādityekārthatvam |
atha kimarthamubhayaṃ sūtritamiti | yeṣāṃ saṃhatānāmekaprayojanatvaṃ teṣāṃ
tadaśaktervyastānāmekāntenaiva sākāṅkṣatvaṃ bhavatītyavācyaṃ tat | atha vā vibhajya -
mānasākāṅkṣatvaṃ saṃhataikaprayojanatvena vinā na bhavati pratyekakāryeṣu nirapekṣa-
pravṛttisiddheḥ I ataḥ sākāṅkṣavibhāgenaivetarākṣepāttanmātramevābhidhāta-
vyamiti |
pratyudāharaṇe darśayati — ucyata iti | puroḍāśe vibhāgaprakāśanamekaṃ
prayojanaṃ nirapekṣāṇāṃ dṛṣṭam | nirvāpe dattottarāpi vibhāganānātvaparicodanā
saṃhatya viśeṣaṇānupādānātpunaḥ punaḥ prayuktasyānuṣaṅgadoṣābhāvādupanyasyate |

prādhānyaṃ tu pratyuktameva | etatkṛto viśeṣaḥ karmānaṅgatvādaraṇīya iti sāmānya-

tatra hi nirvāpaḥ prādhānyena prākāśyo na tasya viśeṣaṇānvayastenārthasyai-

ajitopetam
vinā yadyapyekaprayojanavattvaṃ bhavet tathāpyekāṅgavaikalyādekavākyatvābhāvaḥ, kimuta
yadā rūpabhedādviniyogabhedastadbhedāccādṛṣṭārthatve'pi prayojanabhedaḥ | tvatpakṣe'pi
cāvaśyaṃ yāvadviniyogaṃ tena tena bhavitavyam | iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ, tava samastā-
nmantrānmama vyastebhyaḥ | na caiṣāmadṛṣṭārthatvaṃ brāhmaṇopadiṣṭadṛṣṭārthasādhanatvāt |
tadbalena caiṣāṃ yāvatā vinā coditakaraṇasāmathyaṃ nāsti tāvadanāmnātamapi
kalpanīyaṃ cchinadmītyādi | yadvā yathaiva 'rathaghoṣeṇa māhendrasya stotramupākarotī'
ti vacanādavācakasyāpi viniyogamātrānusandhānena stotrābhimukhyacihnatvaṃ
bhavati, evamiṣetvādīnāṃ chedanādismaraṇahetutvam | ubhayamapi caitatpramāṇavaditi
sūtrakāraiḥ kaiścitkathañcidāśritam | sarvathātraiva madhyava sātavyam | vikalavākya-
prayogāsaṃbhavādavācakaprayogānupapatteścāvyāhāraḥ | atha vā yathāmnātavini
yogānusārāttādṛśenaiva smatu saṃskārādhāne yatitavyam | tatrānuṣṭhātrabhiprāyādhīno-
'nyataraparigraha iti dṛṣṭaprayojanabhedādapi siddho vākyabhedaḥ | kiṃśva -
anyonyanirapekṣāṇāṃ kāryānyatve niyogataḥ |
pratyekaṃ yāgahetutvādyajuṣṭvamavakalpate ||
tathā 'āyuryajñena kalpatām' ityevamādīnāṃ nirākāṅkṣabhinnarūpopa-
labdhernaikatvāpattiḥ satyapyekārthatve | na ca tadasti, āyuḥ kalpanādīnāṃ bhinna-
tvāt nirvāpavadabheda iti cenna | klṛptīriti mantrabahutvacodanāyāḥ pratyakṣa-
tvāt | iha tāvadarthānāmakarmāṅgāpavādavivakṣāyāṃ satyāṃ mantroccāraṇamātraṃ
tad balenaiveti | brāhmaṇopadiṣṭakāryavaśenetyarthaḥ | vikalavākyeti |
śabdānāmarthaviśeṣatvasya sthitatvādavācakasyāprayogādvācakasya ca vikalasyā-
prayogādadhyāhāra iti | yathāmnāteti | viniyogāmnātayostāvanmātraviṣayā-
tvāttāvati ca yathākāryaṃ sidhyati tathā yateteti | vedābhiprāyāvagamātsmaraṇa-
hetusaṃskārādhāne yatitavyam | kiśva yadi saṃhatyārthamabhidadhati padāni vākya-
mityevaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vākyaṃ bubhutsitaṃ syāt tadā syādapi pratyekamavākyatā |
yajustatra vākyamityuktam |

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
prayojanam | atra ca bahvayaḥ klṛptayaḥ śrūyante tadbhedaścārthabhedamantareṇa na bhavatī-
tyakṣaropasarjanabhūto'tha nānātvenāśrīyate | klṛptiviśeṣavacanaṃ ca dṛṣṭārthamiti
mantrabahutvāpādanārthatvāt yathā vibhāga iti | sakṛcchra tanirvāpivailakṣaṇyāt
klṛtīnām punaḥ punaruccaritaśabdo'pi yathā vibhāga: sādhanabhede satyapi na bhidyate,
tathā klṛptibhedo'pi na bhaviṣyatīti manyate | naitadevamityuttaram | iha sāmānya-
vivakṣānupayogāt | atha vā siddhāntavādyeva yathā vibhāge naitadevamiti svaya-
mevaikavākyabhūtamuttaraṃ dadāti | nanu ca vibhāgavākyānāmapi bhedo'styeveti
nopabhyasanīyam | evaṃ tahi naivedaṃ prabhedapratipattyarthaṃ kiṃ tarhi dṛṣṭāntena sahaikatvā-
pādanaparatvāttadīyavikalpapādanāyopanyasyati | yathā vibhāge tathātra vikalpaḥ
kasmānna bhavatīti | naitadevamiti | samānaviṣayatve hi vikalpo bhavati, na
ceha sāmānyavivakṣā, yena vibhāgavadaikārthyaṃ bhavet | klṛptiviśiṣṭāyurāśāsana-
paratvāt vibhāge hi sādhanānāmaprayojanatvātsāmānyabhūtakriyāmātraprakāśana-
paratvam | iha punarāyurādīnāmāśāsyatvena prādhānyāttadviśeṣaṇatvena klṛptyupādānaṃ
na tvāyurādiviśiṣṭā sā prakāśyate niṣprayojanatvāt | athāpi kriyāprādhānyaṃ
tathāpi tadbhedaparatvānnānārthatvam | bhedaparatve ca pūrvoktameva dṛṣṭārthatvamucyate |
atha vā klṛptīrvācayatīti naiva mantraparaḥ klṛptiśabdaḥ, kathaṃ tarhi, kriyābahutvā-
bhighāna eva, śabdānāmarthatantratvāt | ataśca bahutvasaṃkhyayā karmabhedādviśeṣaṇa-
kṛtabhedāśrayaṇācca mantrāṇāṃ tatparatve coditārthanivṛtyādṛṣṭārthatvāt siddhā
bhinnārthaṃtā | kathaṃ punarasminpakṣe vācayatītyucyate, śabdapadārthako hyayaṃ prayujyate |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, ubhayatra prayogāt | yathaiva hi śabdaṃ bruvantaṃ prayuñjāno vācayatī-
atra ca baha mavyaḥ klṛptayaḥ iti | klṛptermantralakṣaṇārthatvādvahavo
mantrāḥ vācayitavyāḥ śrūyanta ityarthaḥ | nirvāsāmye'pi viśeṣavivakṣayā
parigṛhīte vibhāgasāmyāśaṅkāyāṃ bhāṣyakṛduktāyāṃ kāraṇamāha- yathā
vibhāga iti | kluptīnāṃ sakṛcchra tānnirvāpāt punaḥ śrutatvamātreṇa
vailakṣaṇyenāvagamyamānānāṃ śakyo nirvāpavadabhedo vaktuṃ yathā tu vibhāge
punaḥ śruterapi sāmānyavivakṣā tathātra bhavedityāśaṅkṣayata iti |
tadetadvivṛṇoti - punaḥ punariti | prakārāntareṇārthabhedamāha -klṛpti
viśiṣṭa | yurāśāsaneneti | naiva mantraparaḥ klṛptiśabda iti |

[ anuṣaṅgādhikaraṇam ]
jyotiṣṭome tisṛṇāmapyupasadānādāvāgneyī vihitā "agnimanīka"
miti | tatra prathamāyāḥ krame "yā te agne'yāśayā" iti nirākāṅkṣo mantra
āmnāyate | uttarayostu, sākāṅkṣī "yā te agne rajāśayā" "yā te
agne harāśayā" iti | tatra strīliṅgayacchandanirdeśasya strīpadārthena kenacidvinā
paripūrṇatā nāsti | na cāparipūrṇena vyavahāro'vakalpate tatra tadanyathānupapattyā-
vaśyaṃ paripūraṇasamartho vākyaśeṣaḥ kalpanīyaḥ | kutaḥ |
nyūnavākyaprayogāddhi vede naiva samāśritaḥ |
kaścit kvāpi sthitaḥ śeṣaḥ so'nveṣṭavyaḥ prayatnataḥ ||
na hi vaidikavākyānāṃ pratyakṣamevaikaṃ pramāṇaṃ, tadvadeva hyanumānādīnyapi
pramāṇāni | sarvapramāṇapratyastamaye ca ṣaṣṭha enābhāvaḥ paricchidyate | tadiha pratya-
kṣānupalabdhe vākyaśeṣe na tāvatyevoparantavyam | yadyanumānādīnyapi nirvātiṣyante
tata etāvānevāyaṃ mantra iti kalpayiṣyāmaḥ | atha tu taiḥ kaścidavagamyate tatasta-
tatsahito'yaṃ mantraḥ sakalaḥ prāk tasmādūna ityavyavasyāmaḥ | tadihārthāpattyā
tāvatsāmānyataḥ kaścidasti śeṣa ityadhyavaseyam | tataścābhāvasyānavasarādūnamidaṃ
vākyaṃ na prayogārham | tatra viśeṣākāṅkṣāyāṃ laukikaḥ kalpanīya uta
vaidika eva pūrvamantrasthastanūrvaṣiṣṭha tyā diranuṣaktavya iti saṃśaye laukika ityāha |
klṛptārthānāmakarmāṅgabhāve'pi yanmantroccāraṇādadṛṣṭaṃ kalpanīyaṃ tat
klṛptyarthābhidhānādeva kalpyatāmevaṃ mantralakṣaṇaṃ na bhaviṣyati | ādāvāgne-
yīti | āgneyoṣṭiragnimanīkamityādinā vākyena vihiteti | nanvadṛṣṭasi-
dvayarthameva vedāśrito'rthāpattimātreṇa kathaṃ kalpanā mahaṃtīti pṛcchati - kuta iti |
nyūnavākyaprayogāddhīti | svādhyāyavidhirāmnātabhāgasyādhyayanenopādānamāha-
yāvatā tu vinā na tena kāryasiddhistāvadarthāpattisiddhamanujānātyeva | nanu
pratyakṣaka pramāṇasya vedasya tannivṛtyā bhāve'vagate kathamarthāpattyudaya ityāśaṅ-
kyāha- nahi vedavākyānāmiti |

[ abhyāsādhikaraṇam ]

yasyāyaṃ guṇa upavaddho dṛśyate kālavidhipare vākye upāṃśu paurṇamāsyāṃ
yajanniti | na tvetadeva guṇasyotpattivākyamayāgavidhipare satyanekārthavidhi-
prasaṅgāt | ata 'upāṃśuyājamantarā' ityanenaiva prāptasya saṃkīrtanamiti ||11||
'hṛdayamupāṃśuyāja' iti yajñapuruṣasya pradhānaprāyeṇāvayavena saṃstavāt pradhā-
namityavagamyate | kathaṃ punarapradhānatvaṃ pūrvapakṣe prasajyate | ucyate,
trayāṇāṃ vaiṣṇavādīnāṃ samudāye'vadhārite |
agnīṣomīya evaikaḥ paurṇamāsyāṃ vidhīyate ||
tāvabrūtāmagnīṣomāviti yo'gnīṣomadevatyaḥ sa evaikaḥ kālaviśeṣa-
saṃyuktaḥ pūrṇamāsagrahaṇena phale codyate | tatra vaiṣṇavaprājāpatyayorguṇabhāvāt sakalo-
pāṃśuyājaprādhānyavacanānupapattiḥ | nanu tavāpi vaikalpikadoṣatvād devatāsu yadevā-
gnīṣomīya daivatyastadeva prādhānyamiti nityavat pradhānatvasaṃkīrtanaṃ naiva ghaṭate | naiṣa
doṣaḥ |

ūrdhvamāghārayati taṇḍulairjuhotītyādibhirevaṃbhūtā ekādhārā homāśca
vihitā viparivarttante | na hi 'viṣṇurupāṃśu yaṣṭavyaḥ' ityādivad dravyaprādhānyena
yāgahomabhāvanayorabhibhavaḥ | kiñca
na caiṣāmekavākyatvaṃ na ca stutiparā śrutiḥ |
aikāntiko vidhistena guṇavat karmagocaraḥ ||
nopāṃśuyājavākyavadeṣāmekavākyatvamiṣṭaṃ na ca stutyarthatā, tenobhayapakṣe
niścite vidhitve guṇamātraṃ vidhīyatāṃ tadyuktaṃ vā karmeti karmavidhānaṃ yuktam | kutaḥ
vidhitvaṃ pratyayopāttaṃ na nāmapadamṛcchati |
tadvidhirbhāvanātaścet saiva pūrvaṃ vidhīyatām ||
na tāvad davyādiśabdaḥ svayaṃ vidhitvamabhidhīyate | na ca pratyayopāttaṃ
sadbhāvanādhātvarthavyatirekeṇa svatantrairdadhyādibhiḥ saṃbadhyate | na hi bhavati dadhnā
'yāditi | kiṃ tahi daghnā kuryāditi saṃbadhyate | homaṃ vānena kuryāditi |
tasmād yat prasādena dadhyādeḥ karttavyatā tāveva bhāvanādhātvarthau na vidhīyete iti |

darśayitumupakramyate tadaiva pūrvataraṃ bhāvanādhātvarthavidhirvalādāpadyate | tasmāddhoma
dadhyādiviśiṣṭa bhāvanaiva vidhīyate | tataśca tāsāṃ rūpavatīnāṃ pracayaśiṣṭaḥ
samudāyo'stītyagnihotraṃ juhotīti tadanuvādo vijñāyate | nimittaphalacodanayośca
ekatvopajanaḥ samudāyānuvāde prayojanam | evaṃ ca sati davyādebhinnakarmaviṣaya-
tvādanyāyyo vikalpo na prasajyate | tayordhvamāghārayatītyādiṣu yadyapi dravyadevataṃ
na śrūyate tathāpyupāṃśutvanyāyena vyavacchedakāraṇatvādūrdhvatvādibhireva rūpavattāyāṃ
kalpitāyāmāghāramāghārayatītyarūpaḥ san svārthe viniyoktumaśaknuvannanuvādo
bhavati | tasyāpi prayojanam 'indra3 ūrdhvo adhvara' iti mantra viniyoge, 'tasyā-
ghāramāghāryam' iti ca dravyavidhāvekatvasiddhiḥ ||13||

tavyaḥ | idaṃ ca bhāvārthādhikaraṇe'bhihitaṃ yayā na dhātvarthaḥ sādhyatvena vidhīyata
iti | na hyakaraṇabhūto'sau sācyāpekṣatvād vākyāntareṇāpi phale vidhātuṃ śakyeta |

kahāyanyādivatparaspareṇāsaṃbadhya bhāvanāsaṃbaddhānāmuttarakālaṃ parasparopakāri-
tvam | tathā ca saptamanavamādyayorvakṣyatīti na viśiṣṭadhātvarthavidhānam | bhāvanāṃ
ca prati yāgasya karaṇatvād dravyaṃ prati karmatve sati, 'aikaśabdhe parārthavat' [mī0
sū0 1|4|8|] ityetadāpadyata ityayuktametat | tathā vrīhīna prokṣatītyatra na prokṣaṇena
vrīhīniti kaścitsaṃbandhaḥ | tenobhayoḥ karotyarthaṃ saṃbandhānna dhātvarthī vrīhiṣu vidhī-
tmāpi dhātvartho na sākṣādvidhīyata ityuktamidānīṃ nāso sādhyaḥ sādhya-
bhāvanāviśeṣaṇatvena | ye tu tadanurañjanasamarthā viśeṣā ityādinā bhāvārthā-
dhikaraṇa uktatvādityāha - idaṃ ca bhāvārthādhikaraṇa iti | 'arthābhidhāne ca
bhāvanāviśeṣaṇatvenaiva vidheyo na svata iti pūrvameva sādhyaṃ tatra heturna
hya karaṇeti | phale hi vidheyavidhiḥ sādhyasvabhāvasya ca vyāpārātmano
bhāvyākāṅkṣiṇaḥ sākṣāt sa syādasādhyastvasau dhātvarthī yadi sādhyānvayinīṃ
bhāvanāṃ karaṇatvena na viśinaṣṭi na tadā phale vidhātuṃ śakyate | tathā hi
ākhyātayoginā tāvadvākyena vidheḥ śabdato bhāvanānvayānna śakyate |
bhāvanābhidhānarahitenāpi yaṣṭavyamityādineti |
anyā vyākhyā, dhātvartha ākhyātādabhāvyaniṣṭhaḥ sādhyarūpaḥ pratīyate |
bhāvanā tu bhāvyaniṣṭheti sthitiḥ | tatra yadi dhātvarthasiddhayaṃ puruṣo niyujyeta
syādapi tasya vidhiḥ | idaṃ tu nirākṛtaṃ vyartha vidhyasambhavāt | na cāgnihotraṃ
juhotīti sādhyatvena vihitasyāgnihotraṃ juhuyāt svargakāma iti vākyāntareṇa
phalāmvayādvidhyupādānaṃ yato karaṇabhūto'sau svavākye sādhyatvāt sādhyāntarā-
napekṣopekṣayā cotpattiviniyoga vidhyorekavākyatā | ato naivaṃ viniyoga-
vidhirutpatteranyena samarpaṇād niyogamātre vyāpriyate | tasmāt karaṇatvena
vidheyaḥ | karaṇatvaṃ ca kriyāgvayitvamiti |
abhyā vyākhyā, nahītyādeḥ sādhyatvena dhātvarthavidhānena vidhānaṃ
svatastasya kimiti phalena vidhānamityata āha-na kriyāṃ karaṇatvena-
viśiṣan yena svarūpeṇāsādhyānapekṣatvāt śrutaphalenāpi vākyena vidhātuṃ
atathāvidhāne ca ā0 mā0 |

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya

samvattikam
[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
juhoteriti spaṣṭamapi dhātuccāraṇamanavabudhya sakalākhyātānuvādābhiprāyeṇāha -
yadi juhotītyanuvāda iti | dadhiśabdeneti mā vocata iti | vājapeyādhikaraṇa-
vad granthacchāyā yojanīyā | pratyayo vidhireva dhātvartho'nuvāda iti vivekaḥ |
yadi vidhāyakāviti - bhāvanāyāṃ prāptāyāṃ kathaṃ vidhānamityabhiprāyaḥ | vākyārtho
yastaṃ vidhātumiti | dadhnaḥ padārthatvānnaiva vākyārthatvaṃ saṃbhavatītyetadapi dadhi-
viśiṣṭabhāvanābhiprāyameva | tasmāt karmāntaracodane iti - upāṃśuyājavadeva
vyākhyeyam | juhotyāghārayatilakṣitāsādhāraṇārthaviṣayatvācca saṃjñopabandha-
siddhiḥ | iha vidhīyamānatvena ca karmānnarebhyo vyavacchedaḥ | prakaraṇācca dravya-
daivataṃ lapsyata iti nārūpatvam | na cotpattivākye kathaṃbhāvo nāsti phalasaṃbandhaṃ
tu yāvatpratīkṣiṣyate | na cāvihitadravyadevatasya phalavidhyanupapattiḥ | homamātratayā
vihitasya paścādapi tadgrahaṇāvirodhāt | iṣyate caivamādo saṃnidhivaśenāpyanu-
vāda: | prāptyapekṣo hāsau yathā kathañcitpratyabhijñānaṃ prārthayate | na ca para-
prakaraṇe pāṭhādāghārasyāvāntaraprakaraṇalopaḥ | tasyāpi hyākhyātapratyayavaśena
ki kena kathamityastyapekṣā | tenaṃ śakyaṃ prakaraṇena dravyadevatāvidhānam | bahūni
cādṛṣṭādi tvayā kalpyāni | na ca taṇḍulādihomānāmūrdhvādyādhārāṇāṃ vā vihitā
dadhiśabde sati kena vidhīyata ityākṣepo dadhiśabdasya vidhiśaktya-
bhāvābhiprāyaḥ | sa caivaṃ vyañjanīya ityāha- dadhiśabdenetīti | yadi
vājapeyaguṇavidho yajetetyanuvādaḥ kenedānīṃ guṇo vidhīyeta | vājapeyaśabdeneti
mā vocaḥ | nahi nāma padānāṃ vidhiśaktirastīti tatra granthastannyāyo-
'trāpi draṣṭavya iti | viśiṣṭabhāvanābhiprāyamevetyevakāreṇa darśitamidamapi
bhāṣyaṃ viśiṣṭavidhimeva viśeṣaṇaphalamāha | na dhātvarthānuvādena viśeṣaṇa-
vidhānam | tasya padārthatvena vākyārthatvānupapapatteriti | phalavāvayaṃ tu
yāvaditi | phalavākyeneyamutpattiḥ phale viniyujyata ityavagamyāpekṣite
tatrāṅgānīti | na ca taṇḍulādihomānāmiti | yathāsaṃkhyena homānāṃ
na devatā, ādhārāṇāṃ devatā, na dravyamiti | na cordhvādyādhā-

ekatra vedṛśe guṇe śrūyamāṇe vidhiśakti: saṃkrāmatītyagnihotre'bhihitam |
ato na karmānuvādabuddhirapaitītyanuvādatvaṃ yajeḥ | tatra somaśabdena dravyameva
gṛhṇātinā sāmānyākṣiptaṃ niyamyate |
atha vā ‘somaṃ krīṇāti’ 'somamabhiṣuṇoti' 'somaṃ pāvayati' ityādibhiḥ
prākaraṇikairguṇavākyaiḥ somarase kalpite gṛhṇāticodanāḥ pravṛttā iti |
somo na vidhīyate | tataśca svābhāvikakaraṇatvayuktayāgasāmānādhi-

nanu

tu tadeva prayojanaṃ yatprayogavākyagataikatvopajananaṃ nāma | tasmādanuvāda iti |

ityucyate | tanna, anaṅgisome saṃyuktānāṃ prakaraṇaviniyogāsambhavāt |
ataḥ somatvakārthasamavāyi prakṛtāpūrvasādhane viniyogo'ṅgīkartavyaḥ | na ca
viniyuktasthāpūrva sādhanatvam | atha saṃskāravākyaiva niyogasaṃgamamanicchadbhi-
raṅgabhāvaḥ somasyākṣipyata ityucyate | hanta, sa evāyamuktaḥ somena yajeteti |
nanu yāgavidhipūrvakastadanuvādena somavidhiḥ | yāgavidhiśva rasopa-
kalpaka saṃskāravidhyapekṣaḥ | te ca vidhayaḥ somavidhipūrvakā iti cakrakamā-
padyeta | ataḥ saṃskāravidhyākṣepalabhya eva somāṅgabhāvo na pratyakṣavacanāva-
bodhya iti |
1
atrocyate | arthādapi yāge'ṅgabhāvaḥ pūrvasiddhaḥ kalpanīyaḥ | tataḥ saivā-
nupapattiḥ saṃskārebhyaḥ prāgyāgābhāvāt | ata evaṃ vaktavyaṃ parasparopapāda-
kārthā nityā amī vidhayaḥ | tatra yasya yatrāṃśe vyāpṛtistatrāsau vidhi-
ritarasādhye'nuvādaḥ pratītistvadhyayanakramād bhavatu kenāpi prakāreṇa |
ayaṃ vācyaḥ pratītikramaḥ | somavidhiryāgapratītimapekṣate | na dravyaviśeṣa-
nirūpaṇam | anirūpite ca dravyaviśeṣe'styutpattivākye dravya devatā-
sambandhāvagamād yāgapratītiḥ somāṅgabhāvaṃ cāpekṣya saṃskāravidhayastatra samu-
panipatantīti | atha dāvatīyate tāvadabhidhānataḥ somapradhānebhyaḥ saṃskāra-
vākyebhyo rasaḥ, tasyānvayo yāge | so'sati rasaprakṛtisomāṅgabhāva
pramāṇo'vasīdannanena somaviniyogena samarthyata iti | yadi ca somāṅga-
bhāvamanapekṣya saṃskāravidhayaḥ pravartante | tadā somasya saktuvadasaṃskārā-
rhatvāt dravyakarmasaṃyoganyāyena somaguṇākā abhiṣavādividhayaḥ syuḥ |
tatrābhiṣavādayaḥ prakṛtā, na somaḥ, guṇatvāt | ataḥ kathaṃ tasya taddhitenā-
bhidhānam | athābhidhānaparyavasānāya guṇabhūtasyāpi parāmarśaḥ | so'pi na,
gṛhṇātītyanena vyavadhānāt | na cāsya somarasaviṣayatvaṃ, kāraṇābhāvāt |
na hāsau prakṛta ityuktam | tena śoṇaśabdenaivāśvasya dhārāśabdeneva tadvacanā-

tvevaṃ prakṛtyapekṣā ki prakṛtistasiddhau vyāpārayitavyā somarasamātraṃ

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
guṇārthena saṃbhavati, utpattivākyaśiṣṭapaśulatāvirodhāt | atha vā pūrvoktena
nyāyenānyata eva paśusomayoḥ siddhatvādasmin prakaraṇe punaranarthako dravyasaṃyogastva-
tpakṣe bhavet | mama tu somaśabdo nāmadheyamagnīṣomīyavākyaṃ ca devatā vidhānārtha-
miti na kiñcidanarthakam | tasmādavadyatigṛha, ṇātisaṃyuktaṃva yāgotpatticodanā
syāt || 17||
naivaito samudāyānuvādo 'codanā vākṛtasvāt' [mī0 sū0 2|2|10|]
ityetasmādeva hetoḥ | kathaṃ prakṛtā na saṃbhavanti | yato'vadyatigṛhṇātayo na
yāgānāṃ codakāḥ, kiṃ tarhi, saṃskāramātravidhayaḥ | tanmātraparyavasānāt |
tanmātraparyavasāyini vākye niṣpramāṇakatvānnāśrutayāgakalpanaṃ śakyam | evaṃ hi
sa kalpyamānaḥ kalpyeta devatoddeśena gṛhītaṃ dravyaṃ na yāvadyāgena saṃbadhyate na
tāvat taddevatyaṃ kṛtyaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavatīti | tatra yadevānyathānupapattyā yāgaṃ kalpa-
yitumārabhate tadaiva saṃnihitaḥ pratyakṣo yajiraviśeṣātsarvān dravyadevatāsaṃyogānu-
papādayitumupatiṣṭhan yāgāntarakalpanāpramāṇaṃ niruṇaddhi | tasmiścāpramite kasya
samudāyānuvādo bhaviṣyati | yaduktamāgneyādivannāparikalpite yajī vākyānta-
rāpekṣeti | tatra brūmaḥ |
C
puṃvyāpāraniyogena vākyaṃ sarvatra pūryate |
tadalābhe tvapūrṇatvaṃ tallābhe vākyasaṃgatiḥ ||
stadapekṣitāṃ prakṛti samarpayedapīti nātivirodhaḥ | siddhānte tyetadviparī-
tamiti | atha vā pūrvoktena nyāyeneti | yadā somenetyutpattistadevendravāya+
vādivākyaiḥ saṃskāravākyopanītasomarasaviṣayairdevatāvat somaraso'pi
yāgāyopanetuṃ śakyata ityanarthakaḥ someneti tadvidhiḥ | evaṃ mantravarṇa-
nirūpitapaśuprakṛtikahṛdayādiviśiṣṭāvadātavākyaiḥ prakṛtinirjñāta homaprayo-
janaiḥ paśuryāgāṅgatvena bodhayituṃ śakyata ityutpattivākye'narthakaḥ paśusaṃyoga
iti | mama tu somaśabda iti | mamotpattivākyanirūpitaṃ pravṛttinimittaṃ
paścāt pravartamānaṃ nāma labhate | tava tutpannabhāvipravṛttinimittamutpatti-
samaye avidyamānaṃ notpattisthena nāmnā labhyata ityabhiprāyaḥ | tataśca
balād guṇavidhiraṅgīkartavyaḥ sa cānarthaka ityuktam | paśuśabdo'pi mamānu-
vādaḥ | tava ca prāptisamayapravṛtto vidhiḥ syāditi | vākyasaṅgatiriti |
tasyaiva vākyasya saṅgatārthatā samāptiriti yāvat | atha vākyāntarasaṅgatiḥ

[ 195
āgneyo'ṣṭākapālo bhavatīti na yāgādanyaḥ puṃvyāpāraḥ pratīyata iti
prāktadanumānādaparipūrṇaṃ vākyam | iha tvandravāyavaṃ gṛhṇātīti grahaṇabhāvanāyāṃ
puruṣo niyujyate | tatra prāgapi yajyanumānādasti vidheviṣaya iti samāpyate
vākyam | idaṃ tvanyat, yattataḥ paramapi kiñcidapekṣyate | sarvameva hi vākyaṃ
svārthe paryavasite arthāntarāpekṣāṃ na jahāti, na ca tāvatā tadasamāptaṃ bhavati |
yā tvasāvapekṣā sā vākyāntareṇa pūrayiṣyata ityavagamānna vākyāntarasaṃbandhaṃ na
sahate | tena yajyanumānavelāyāṃ prativākyaṃ somena yajeta, ityasyopasthānaṃ na
virudhyate | 'āgneyo'ṣṭākapāla' iti tu bhavaterapuruṣavyāpārātmakatvāt prāgyajerna
kiñcidvihitaṃ na kiñcit pratiṣiddhamityasamāpte vākye vākyāntare cānapekṣite
yajiranumīyamāno na vākyāntaragataḥ saṃvadhyata iti vaiṣamyam | atha yadukta-
mutpattiśiṣṭayoḥ paśusomayorna vākyāntaragatā vikārāpattiryukteti | tatra
brūmaḥ |
dravyeṇātyantabhinnena saha cintyaṃ balāvasam |
hṛdayādirasadvāramaṅgatvaṃ
paśusomayoḥ ||
yadyatrotpattiko paśusomau parityajyātyantavyatiriktaṃ kiñcid dravyaṃ
vidhīyate tato virodhaḥ syādiha tu paśunaiva hṛdayādidvāreṇa yāgaḥ sādhyate |
somena ca rasadvāreṇa na cetikartavyatāsaṃbandhe kaścid virodhaḥ | paśusomo hi
yāgasādhanatvena codyamānāviti kartavyatāmā kāṅkṣataḥ | tatra ca rasahṛdayādi-
niṣpattidvāreṇetyevaṃ kathaṃbhāvaḥ pūryate, tenānugrāhakatvānna tatkalpane virodhā-
pattiḥ | yattu prākaraṇikaiḥ krayādivākyaireva prāptatvādiha dravyasaṃyogo'narthaka
iti | tatrāpi acodakāśca saṃskārā [mī0 sū0 2/2/18 | ] ityevottaram |
na hi te vidhayaḥ somasya paśorvā codakāḥ kiṃ tarhi tayoreva saṃskārakāḥ |
tena coditasya dravyasya yathākathañcidupādānena yāgasādhanatve prasakte

rvyāpāro bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkyedamucyate | atha vā 'yajyapekṣāyāmapi satyāṃ
vākyāntarasambandho gṛha jāti vākye bhaviṣyatyatra kasmānna bhavatītyā-
śaṅkṣayeti | na ca mantravarṇaṃsyeti | na ca vaktavyaṃ hṛdayādibhiḥ prāṇidravyasāmānya-

syātyantāvihitacchāgaprāpaṇasāmathyaṃ paśucodanā vihitasya tu sandehāpanayana-
mātramupayujyate' | na cāvadyatirekāntena yāgamanumāparyāta | vināpi tena khaṇḍana-

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
tulyārthāśca vrīhiyavādayo vikalpante | vākyasaṃyogino hi te parasparanirapekṣāḥ
svena svena vākyena viniyujyante | nanu 'aindravāyavaṃ gṛhṇāti' iti vākyameva
viniyojakamupalabhyate | ataśca 'maitrāvaruṇam' ityetasya pūrvanirapekṣameva devatā-
vidhisāmathyaṃ vijñāyate | satyam | grahaṇaṃ pratyabhyonyanirapekṣā vidhāyante na tu
tatraikārthatvamadṛṣṭārthatvāt | kathamadṛṣṭārthatvamiti cet | ucyate,
pūrva pradhānasaṃbaddha saṃskāre śrūyate yadi |
tad dṛṣṭārthaṃ vinā tena yadi vāsau na sidhyati ||
yadi hi prathamaṃ yāgena saṃbaddhāḥ satyo devatā nirvāpāvāhanādiṣviva grahaṇeṣu
punaḥ śrūyeran yadi vā tābhirvinā grahaṇameva na nirvarteta tatastatra śravaṇaṃ dṛṣṭārtha-
manāgatasmṛtirūpeṇa kalpeta | na vivāyvādayaḥ prathamaṃ jyotiṣṭome śrutāḥ |
nāpi tadabhāvena grahaṇaṃ sidhyati | tatraivaṃ ca satyapi yaddevatāyuktaṃ grahaṇaṃ kartavya -
miti śrūyate tatrādṛṣṭādṛve na kiñcit prayojanamasti | nanu ca yāgasyatā devatā
bhaviṣyantīti jñātvā vināpi prathamaviniyogena tadviṣayasmṛtyarthatvaṃ vijñāyate |
naitadasti | kutaḥ ?
1
sarveṇa vidhivelāyāṃ prayojanamapekṣyate |
tadbalāyānataḥ kalpyo dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthanirṇayaḥ ||

bhavati | tatra yadyapi prakaraṇavaśāditikartavyatātmakasya grahaṇasya yāgasaṃbandha:
kathamapi bhavatītyavadhāryate tathāpi devatānāmabhyasaṃyuktatvādakriyārūpatvācca na
prakaraṇena yāgārthatvaṃ vijñāyata ityavaśyaṃ yāgasaṃbandhanirapekṣaṃ tāvad grahaṇaṃ prati
prayojanaṃ kalpyamityadṛṣṭakalpanamāpadyate | tasmiṃstu śāstravelāyāmavadhārite yadya-
nuṣṭhānakāle dṛṣṭamapyanuniṣpādi bhavati kena tannivāryate | na tu tena śāstrapravṛttiḥ
pratibadhyate | tena prativākyaṃ devatāsaṃyogānāmadṛṣṭārthatvātsamuccayastatra | na
ca yat samitsaṃbandhena kriyate tat tanūnapātsaṃbandhena ityanena nyāyena bhedena
śrutā devatā grahaṇasaṃbaddhā nopapadyate yadi grahaṇaṃ nāvartate iti pratidevataṃ somo

ca yat samitsambandheneti | samitsambandhātmanā yāgena yad dṛṣṭaṃ kriyate | na

i jee
grahītavyaḥ | eṣa tāvatsūtrārthaḥ | tadbhedāt devatāsaṃyogabhedād grahaṇakarmaṇo'-
bhyāso devatāsuṃyuktagrahaṇa saṃskārya dravya pṛthaktvānadarthakaṃ hi saṃyogāntaraśravaṇamana-
nuṣṭhīyamānamanarthakaṃ syāt | tena grahaṇasya bhedaḥ saṃskāyaṃ dravyaṃ prati guṇabhāvāt |
etaddevatāvanti grahaṇāni yāgaprakramātmakatvānna yāgādṛte devatārthaṃ dravyaṃ saṃskatu
śaknuvantītyevamapekṣite bhaviṣyati kaścidyāgo ya etāni dravyāṇi devatāsaṃbandha-
māpādayiṣyatīti | yāyo'pi cāvihitadevatākaḥ śrutiliṅgavākyairdevatāmalabhamānaḥ
prakaraṇasya cedṛśe vastunyakkriyātmake vyāpārābhāvād grahaṇeṣu tāvadvaniyojakaṃ
bhavati | tāni cāparyāryāvidhānād bhinnādṛṣṭasaṃbandhitvācca yugapat gṛhyante tadvaśena
devatānāmapi samuccayasiddhiḥ | nanu prakaraṇagṛhītānāmapyekārthānāṃ puroḍāśa-
vibhāgamantraprabhṛtīnāmiṣyata eva vikalpaḥ, satyamiṣṭo na tu tatra prakaraṇaṃ kevalaṃ
vyāpriyate | liṅgānumitayā śrutyā pratyekaṃ vibhāgāṅgatve vijñāte darśapūrṇamāsa-
vibhāgārthatvamātraṃ prakaraṇād bhavati vibhāgāntare'pyānarthakyāt | tatra yadyapi darśa-
pūrṇamāsau samuccayena gṛhṇītastathāpi na vibhāgadvāranirapekṣayoḥ sāmarthyamasti |
yacca dvāraṃ tadvikalpenāvaruddhaṃ na samuccayagṛmyam, iha punaviparītameva tat |
yānyeva hi dvārāṇi grahaṇāni teṣveva devatāḥ samuccitāḥ | tatra pratyekaṃ grahaṇa-
yogyo'pi dvārasamarpaṇavaśena pravartamānaḥ kimidānīṃ kariṣyati | nanu grahaṇānāmapi
bhijñānakāraṇamastītyanena samuccetavyatvāt yadi grahaṇaṃ nāvartate, tadā sevena

eṣa tāvaditi | tāvacchabdena yāgābhyāse'pyetadeva sūtramityuktamiti | yāgo-
'pi cātrihitadevatāka iti | atra śānajupapattaye viparīto'ryakramo'labhamāno

unavātakam
[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
pratyekaṃ dravyaprakalpanarūpeṇa devatā samarpaṇena ca pradhānopakārasāmarthyamastīti
vibhāgamantravad vikalpena bhavitavyam | bhavedevaṃ yadi pūrvaṃ nānādevado yāgo'vagataḥ
syāt | tatastadartho'yaṃ dravyaprakalpanabheda ityavagamyate na tvasāvevamavagata iti
prakalpanabhedāmnānamadṛṣṭārthaṃ vijñāyate | avaśyaṃ ca guṇātprakalpanāni bhettavyāni |
na hya ekasminprakalpane yugapatparyāyeṇa vā devatāḥ saṃkalpayituṃ śakyante | yaugapadyaṃ
tāvat kramavartinyā vācā naiva śakyam, adevatābhūta eva samudāyaḥ saṃkalpitaḥ
syāt | na hīndravāyvādisamudāye devatātvaṃ kenaciduktam, pratipadotpannastaddhitaḥ
pratyekaṃ pratīyamānatvāt | na ca krameṇoddeśaḥ | katham ?
dravyasya gṛhyamāṇasya devatoddeśacodanā |
na caikāṃ devatāṃ muktvā taduddeśasya sambhavaḥ ||
yadi tu prāgūrdhvaṃ vā grahaṇāduddiśedacoditameva kuryāt | kiñca -
etasyā iti saṃkalpa uddeśo'trābhidhīyate |
anyasmina kriyamāṇe ca pūrvastatra vipadyate ||
yad dravyamindravāyubhyāṃ dātavyamiti saṃkalpitaṃ tasya tyaktaprāyatvādanīśo
yajamānaḥ kathamiva vakṣyati mitrāvaruṇābhyāmetadeveti | tatrakāmuddiśya devatāntara-
saṃkalpe kriyamāṇe pūrvadevatatastad dravyamācchinnaṃ syāt | pūrvasaṃkalpālocanena vā
naivottaraḥ pāramārthikatvaṃ pratipadyata ityasaṃvyavahāramātramevānuṣṭhīyeta, na codanārthaḥ |
tenāvaśyaṃ yāvaddevataṃ grahaṇabhedaḥ kartavyaḥ | kiñca —
śakyate cāgṛhote'pi yatra kvacidavasthite |
dravye'smin devatoddeṣṭumityadṛṣṭārthatā vidheḥ ||
devatoddeśasvatvatyāgātmako hi yāgaḥ | śakyate ca pūyamānāvasthenāpi
somena krayottarakālaṃ vā sarvāvasthāviyuktenāpi nirvartayitumityaśeṣottara saṃskāra-
vidhānamadṛṣṭārtham | yatra tu puroḍāśādi dravyaṃ codyate tatra yāvadavahantyādividhānaṃ
tatsarvaṃ tanniṣpattyarthatvād dṛṣṭārthaṃ bhavati | na ca 1 grahacamasasthena somena yaṣṭavyami -
tyutpatticodanā, latāmātraparyavasānāt | tenātmīyādṛṣṭabhedanivṛttyarthamapi
|
grahaṇāni samuccetavyāni 'daśamuṣṭīmimāṃte' iti ca prakaraṇe parimāṇaṃ śrutam |
ataḥ 'somena yajeta' ityetadvacanameva viśeṣyate daśamuṣṭinā somena yajeteti | sa ca
madhyadṛṣṭārthamityupapādayati śakyate cagṛhīte'pīti |
1. na ceha | iti kāśī mudritapāṭhaḥ |

[ 201
niyataparimāṇenodakenābhiṣuto'lpaparimāṇeṣu bhinneṣu grahacamaseṣu na grahaṇabheda-
mantareṇa śakyate grahītumiti bhedena gṛhyate | tathāvasyena ca yāgo nirvartayitavyaḥ |
so'pyanabhyastastathāvasthitaṃ na śaknoti sarvaṃ saṃbhāvayitumiti prayojanavaśenā-
bhyasyate, tena tatrāpi sūtram | tadbhedāt prakalpitasomabhedādyāgakarmaṇo'bhyāsaḥ,
svīkṛtadravyapṛthaktvādaśeṣamatyajyamānamanarthakaṃ syāt | ata utpattau sakṛcchra ta-
syāpi vākyāntaraprakalpitātrasthotpattikadravyaguṇatvavaśenānuṣṭhānabhedaḥ | taddhi
dravyamavaśyaṃ yathāgṛhītameva dātavyam | punarekīkaraṇa pūrvakṛtagrahaṇabhedavaiyathyaṃ-
prasaṅgāt | na ca yathāsaṃkalpitāḥ svaṃrbhāgaṃrdevatāḥ saṃbadhyeran | na ca tyāgakāle

sa eva yāgastadevāpūrvaṃ seva devateti niścayānna vyatiriktakalpanaṃ labhyate | tasmā-
tatraiva karmaṇyāmikṣāvājinayo vikalpaḥ samuccayo vā | satyapi cotpattivākya-
śiṣṭatve niṣkṛṣya yāgaṃ guṇāntaraṃ vidhāsyate | yadyapi ca dravyadevatāsaṃyogānu-
mitatvādaso na niṣkṛṣṭaḥ śrutastathāpi kriyātmanāstyevānumānikī vyatireka-
buddhiḥ | yāgapadameva vātrānumīyate | tadāmikṣāparityāge'pi na vinaśyati |
vājiśabdo'pi ca tadanumitayāgapratipattyarthamevoccāryate | na hi tannirapekṣo-
vājivājinasaṃbandho'vakalpate | kārakāṇāṃ parasparasaṃbandhābhāvāt | tasmānna
karmāntaramiti | atrābhidhīyate |
guṇāntarāvaruddhatvāt pūrvakarmaṇyasaṃbhavāt |
kriyāntaramatiṃ kuryādātmasaṃgataye guṇaḥ ||
1
apūrva: karmāntarasaṃyogānarho vā guṇaḥ karmāntaraṃ kalpayati | vākyayoḥ
samatvāt, itaretaranirapekṣatvāt | ubho hi dravyadevatāsaṃyogāvātmavirodhe-

12|3|10]' iti hi sthāsyatyetat | vikalpa evāstviti cet, na | atulya-

saṃbandhaḥ, vājinasaṃbandhastu vākyena | sarvatraiva tāvat-

devatā saṃgatiḥ śrutyā taddhitāntātpratīyate |
caturthyantāttu vākyena padadvayasamīpataḥ |
[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
vaiśvadevīti hi śrutyaiva devatāsaṃbandhapratīteḥ 1 | itaratra tu 'vānibhyo
vājinam' iti padadvayasāmīpyādgasyate | nantrihāpyāmikṣāpadasaṃnidhānavaśenaiva
dravyadevatāsaṃbandhaḥ pratīyata iti tulyaṃ vākyagamyatvaṃ bhavet | atha vaiśvadevīti
śrutyā devatātvaṃ vākyena dravyasaṃbandhaḥ sa ihāpyaviśiṣṭaḥ | caturthīśrutyā devatā-
tve'bhihite vākyena dravyasaṃganimātrakaraṇāt | atha dravyamātra saṃbandhe taddhinā-
ntenaiva kṛte viśeṣamātra māmikṣā gdasaṃnidherbhaviṣyatītyucyate | tadayaviśiṣṭa.
mitaratrāpi | vājisya ityekapadenaiva saṃpradānaṃ bruvatā saṃpradeyādinā tadanu-
papatteḥ sāmānyākṣepe sati vājinaṇdasaṃnidhānena viśeṣamātrapratīteḥ | api
ca bhavatu nāma taddhitaśrute dravyamātra saṃbandhaḥ | tatra dravyamānaṃ vājine'pya viśiṣṭa-
miti na virudhyate | viśeṣayostvāmikṣāvājinayovirodhaḥ | tayośvobhayatra
vākyalabhyasaṃbandhatvādaviśeṣaḥ | tena vikalpaḥ prāpnotyeva | taducyate
naiva hi dravyamānasya taddhitairdevatocyate |
asya śabdābhidheyasya viśeṣasyaiva devatā ||
viśve devā devatā'syā iti hi na sarvanāmnā dravyasāmānyamabhidhīyate |
kiṃ tahi, saṃnihito viśeṣaḥ, sa eva vyavahārakṣamo nānirūpitaṃ dravyamātram |
ataḥ prātipadikena yo'rtha upāttaḥ sa pramāṇāntaragamyasya dravyaviśeṣasya devate -
tyetat taddhitena śrutyaivocyate | tadaścāntarṇīta saṃbandhadevatopasarjanadravyaviśeṣa-
pratyaye padāntaranirapekṣeṇa taddhitāntena padena nirvartite kaḥ punarasī dravyaviśeṣo
dānamityevaṃ śuddhameva karmāsya pratipādyaṃ na viśiṣṭamiti | sa eva vyavahāra-
kṣama iti | viśvedevā devatā asya yaduddeśe vyavahāre saṃpraveyatāṃ kṣamate
so'rthastaddhitasya viśeṣaśca yathā na dravyamātramiti | tataśvāntanirṇīta-
saṃbandheneti | yadyapyabhidheyo'pi dravyaviśeṣo na tāvat pratīyate tathāpyetat
tad devatyaṃ dravyamiti taddhitena nirvartitaṃ tat kimityapekṣyamāṇaṃ padāntarāt
tatsvarūpameva kevalaṃ pratipadyate na tasya tad devatyatvam | pūrvamevābhidhānā-
vagatatvāt | viśeṣopanayapūrvikāpi tasya devatāmbavapratītirbhavantī
taddhitārthābhidhānādeva bhavati na vākya iva pratītapadārthadvayasthānabhihita-
saṃbandhakalpanā samabhivyāhāropapattaye bhavatīti |
1. pratyāyitaḥ iti kāśī mudrita pāṭhaḥ |

tena yaivāmikṣā saiva devatāsaṃvandhaṃ yāsyatītyevamukkā satī vaiśvadevīśabde -
nocyate | tena śabdayorevātra sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ nārthayoḥ | itaratra tu-
na prātipadikenoktaṃ na vibhaktyā haviḥ svataḥ |
na vāsya devatāyoga iti vākyāt pratīyate ||
vājibhya iti tu nātra prātipadikena vibhaktyā vobhābhyāṃ vā dravya-
sāmānyaviśeṣatatsaṃbandhānāmanyatamamapyabhidhīyate | kiṃ tahi prātipadikārtha :

ityavaśyaṃ vākyenaiva vājinapadasamīpyātpratyāyayitavyaḥ | api ca
'yathehāsya padārtho'pi taddhite'ntargataḥ smṛtaḥ |
1
na saṃpradānamasyeti caturthyevaṃ vidhīyate ||

pādīyeta | kiṃ tahi, saṃpradānamātrameva tadabhidheyatvenoktaṃ tathā ca pratīyate |

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
na ca saṃpradānasyaiva devatātvaṃ mā bhūt 'hiraṇyamātreyāya dadāti' ityatrātreyo
devatā | yadi ca devatā sampradānaṃ syāttato yāgadānayorabheda eva syāt |
pratigrahe vyāpṛtaṃ ca kārakaṃ sampradānaṃ bhavati | na ca devatāyāḥ pratigṛhītṛtvaṃ
saṃbhavati | tenāvaśyaṃ yaḥ sampradānatvāt prāgbhāvī taduddeśaḥ, sa caturthyā
lakṣayitavyaḥ | yadvā parasvatvāpādanasaṃbandhottarakālabhāvyaiśvarya, tādātmya
prasiddherdevatātvasya | yadyapyanirākaraṇātmaka sampradānameva devatetyabhyupa-
gamastathāpi sāmānyena vyabhicāriviśeṣalakṣaṇādviprakṛṣṭatvam | api ca
caturthīpañcamī rūpasandehādviprakṛṣṭatā |
taddhitasya tvasandehānna buddhiviprakṛṣyate ||
vājibhya iti rūpasāmānyāt prasaktaṃ sadyāvat prañcamītvamapanīya caturthītvaṃ
sādhyate tāvanmativikṣepaḥ | nanu taddhite'pyane kāpatyādyartha sandehāt tulyametat |
viṣama upanyāsaḥ | śabdasandeho mayopanyastaḥ, tvayā'rthasaṃdehaḥ | sarvathā tāvat
taddhitatvamasandigdham | arthaḥ punaryogyatvāditarāsaṃbhavena devatātmako nirṇeṣyate |
bhavatastu nirṇīte'pi śabde caturthyarthānekatvādupapadakārakalakṣaṇasandehādastyeva
viprakarṣaḥ | tenārthasandeha ubhayoḥ samatvānnaikaścodyate | śabdasandehastvasādhā-
raṇatvāccoditaḥ | kiñca -
sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ hi bhinnapravṛttinimittayoḥ svaviṣayānatilaṅghane naikapara-
tayā bhavatītarattu svaviṣayānvita padārthāntaraparatayā | seyamamvayapratītiḥ
paraviṣayapratīterupari bhavantī viprakarṣamāpādayatīti | yā bhūt hiraṇyamātre
yāyeti | tatra ko doṣaḥ, prasiddhivirodhaḥ | na hāsau devateti prasiddha iti |
yadi ca devateti | yadi devatā pratigṛha ṇīyāt tadavasāna evaṃ tyāgo' yāgaḥ
syāt | na tadā dānād bhidyeteti | taduddeśa iti | viśeṣaṇapradhānametattaccha-
bdavācyoddeśakarma tālakṣaṇīyetyartho yato devatālakṣaṇīyeti pratijñāvyutpādana-
miti | nanu pratigrahādaiśvayaṃ bhavatyatastadabhāve devatāyāḥ kathaṃ tadata āha-
tādātmyaprasiddheriti | aiśvaryeṇāpi devatātvaṃ lakṣyamuddeśakarmatayaiva syāgaṃ prati
pratītasaṃbandhaśca lakṣayati na vastusaṃbaddhabhāji gamanamivājyāni | prasiddhaṃ ca
mantrārthavādalokeṣu prattaṃ dravyaṃ pratyeśvaryātmanā devatātvam | ato devatātva-
'pratipattiparayā caturthyā svārthāvinābhūtamaiśvaryaṃ lakṣyata iti |

āmikṣotpadyamānena karmaṇā saha yujyate |
tato vākyāntaropāttamutpannena tu vājinam ||
[ 21
yathā cotpattiśiṣṭasya valīyastvaṃ na ca kriyāmātraṃ niṣkṛṣya guṇāntaraṃ
vidhātuṃ śakyate tathoktaṃ citrādhikaraṇe vaiśvadevādhikaraṇe ca | vaiśvadevādhikaraṇa-
nyāyena ca prakaraṇamapekṣitavyamaparaṃ vājinavākyena | kutaḥ ?
dravyadevata' saṃbandho
yāgamātramapekṣate |
āmikṣāyāga eveti tatra prakaraṇād bhavet ||
vājinaṃ hi vinā yāgena vājibhirna saṃbadhyata iti kañcidyāgamapekṣate |
tatra yadāmikṣāyāgasyaiva tadapyaṅgamiti kalpyate tatprakaraṇāt | tataśca yadyapi
vākyenaivāmikṣāyāge viśve devā vidhīyante tathāpyanyanirapekṣa vihitatvādāmikṣāyāḥ
prakaraṇasāpekṣavākyavihitādvājinād balīyastvaṃ bhavediti viśeṣaḥ | prakaraṇā
napekṣayāgasaṃbandhe cānyeṣāmihāpratīterapūrvayāgaḥ saṃbandhī bhavatīti bhedaḥ | na
caivamādīnāmanupāttapuruṣavyāpārāṇāmananumite yāge dākyāntarāpekṣāstītyukta-
māgneyādiṣu |
viśve devāśca rūḍha va saṃvadhyante payasyayā |
vājinena tu saṃbandho bhavedavayavānugaḥ ||
vājina iti hyavayavaprasiddhyā viśve devāḥ pratyāyyante | vākyārthavaccā-
vayavārthī samuccitya saṃvandhaṃ kalpayitvā sā bhavantī śrutimātropanipātalabhyāyāḥ
samudāyaprasiddherdurbalā bhavatīti durbalataraḥ saṃyogo vājinasya |
vājiśabdo'pi vāśvādo sunirūḍhaḥ svabhāvataḥ |
kleśenaiva vinā kāryādviśvedeveṣu vartate ||
devatātvaṃ nāma yasyaiva śabdenoktaṃ tasyaiva yogyasyāyogyaraya vā bhavatīti
na jātinimittaṃ yenāyaṃ vājiśabdo balādanyatra nīyeta | ataśca vājiśabdo'-
śvānāṃ devatātvaṃ vadan vinā kāryeṇa durbalayāvayavaprasiddhayā viśvedeveṣu kalpyata
iti viprakarṣaḥ |
yogyasyāyogyasya veti | anyatra devatātvena pratīteravagatayogyatva-
syāgnyāderanavagatasya vā yogyatveneti |
1. saṃyogo iti mudrita pustaka ṭippaṇīdhūtapāṭhaḥ |

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
tacca karmaṇo nyāyyamiti | tatra kiṃ dṛṣṭaṃ hi karmaṇaḥ phalamiti praśnasya dvidhā-
pratibhāsaḥ | ekastāvanneti brūmaḥ | na hya etad dṛṣṭenānena sidhyatītyādenirā-
karaṇaparatvena siddhāntacchāyānusārāt kimityetāvāneva praśnaḥ | kimityeta-
nnyāyyamityarthaḥ | itara māha-dṛṣṭaṃ hi karmaṇaḥ phalamiti | tasyottaraṃ neti brūma-
iti | yadītyādi tāvat svayamevāśaṅkaya nirākaroti yāvattasmānnaivaṃ jātīyake-
ṣvetad bhavatīti | tatra tu kathaṃ tahi homānnyāyyaṃ phalamityaya maikāntika: siddhānta-
vādinaḥ praśnaḥ | sa cātmīyavacanopasaṃhārādanantaraṃ duḥ śliṣṭaḥ pratibhātītye-
ṣaiva granthayojanā yuktatarā | tacca karmaṇo nyāyyamiti pūrvapakṣavādinābhihite
siddhāntavādī dadhipadānarthakyabhayena bhāvārthādhikaraṇanyāyāsambhavācchabdasyā-
sāmarthyaṃ manyamānaḥ pṛcchati - kiṃ dṛṣṭaṃ hi karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ kṛṣyādita ityanena
sāmānyato dṛṣṭena homāt phalaṃ manyasa iti | yadyapi ca codanālakṣaṇadharmābhyupa-
gamādanumānādīnāmīdṛśeṣvartheṣvasāmarthya sthāpitaṃ tathāpi vedārthanirṇaye teṣāṃ
kāraṇatvāniṣedhāt tadarthanirṇayārthamanumānopanyāsaḥ | paraḥ punarbhāvārthādhikaraṇa-
vāsitāntaḥkaraṇa ākṣipannārha - mā me adhyāropamevaṃ dāḥ | śabde sati nahyanumānaṃ
bravīmi | na caitena sāmānyato dṛṣṭenāsmadabhimataṃ sidhyati | asiddhānaikāntika-
viruddhādidoṣaduṣṭatvādanumānasya | katham ? yadi tāvaddhomaḥ phalavān phalavattayā
dṛśyamānatvādityucyate tataḥ pratijñātārthaṃ kadeśatvādasiddhatvam | atha kṛṣyādīnāṃ
phalavattayā dṛśyamānatvāditi tato'pyapakṣadharmatvam | atha vinaiva pakṣadharmatvād
yadṛcchāmātreṇa kalpyeta tatastadravyeṣvaviśiṣṭam | atha sādṛśyadvāreṇo-
pamayā kalpyeta tacca sādṛśyaṃ phalavadbhiḥ kṛṣyā dibhiḥ saha homasya kriyātmanā
vidyate na dravyasyākriyātmakatvāditi | tatra dravyasyāpyastyeva kiñcit
sādṛśyamiti manasi kṛtvā yadi pratyāsa nāvāntarasādṛśyāpekṣayā tannirākriyate
tataḥ śakyaṃ pratyāsannatarāpekṣayā homasyāpi nirākartumiti manyamāna āha-
homo'pyasadṛśaḥ sādhanasvarūpaphalabhedātkṛṣeḥ |
atha kiñcit sādṛśyaṃ gṛhyate tadravyasyāpi sadanityaṃ dravyavatkāryami
tyādidharmabhedādastīti pūrvābhiprāyavivaraṇam | atha dravyādanyat sadṛśataramastīti-
atha sādṛśyadvāreṇeti | yasya matamupamānaṃ prasiddha dharmānvayena pravṛttama-
prasiddhadharmāvaye'pi pramāṇamiti | tadabhiprāyeṇedamucyate | svayaṃ tu pratiṣetsya-

pūrvasyaiva guṇādindriyavat paśavaḥ phalamiti | kiṃ tāvat prāptaṃ samastenaiva

vinaiva pratyayāvṛttyā kṛtvā śabdaḥ pṛthak śrutiḥ ||
yastu revatīvāravantīya saṃbandhotpattividhānāt tasya ca phalaṃ prati vidhānā-

guṇaṃ vidadhad yadanyatrājñātaśeṣatvaṃ vidhatte | revatyaśca saptamīnirdeśād vāravantīya-
śeṣabhūtatvātprayojana ntaramanapekṣamāṇā na phallena saṃbadhyante | tadguṇakaṃ tu

svayameva vedeneti | āvṛtyaiva vedena karotyartho'bhihito na ca vākyabhedaḥ |
kṛtvā śabda gṛhītasya sambandhasyetaraśeṣatvenāvagamāt svarūpavidhirapyasya phala-
sambandhavidhiśeṣasyārthākṣepāditi |
samprati sambandhasaṃbandhināṃ kasya phale vidhiḥ kasya vā taccheṣatva-
miti nirūpayati - tatrāpyādhāranirdeśāditi | nanu yāgavad vāravantīya-

kuryāt | atha yāge prāptirna phale tena tatra vidhiryāgasyāpi syāt | satyaṃ,
yadi prāpteravidhi brūmaḥ kintu viśeṣaṇopādānādekaśaktividhāyakos

vāravantīyaṃ dvitīyānirdeśādavagataprādhānyaṃ karotyavihitāṃ bhāvanāṃ prati

[ 243
yānyekakriyāviśeṣaṇāni bhavanti tāni tayā vidhīyamānayārthā-

klṛptasthānena sāmnaiva revatīsthānakalpanā ||

ataśca prakṛtayāgasaṃbandhastvatpakṣe vāravantīyasya nāsti | paśya-

dvaśena tatsthānāḥ bhavanti | sevamagniṣṭomasāmavākye'sminnanūhitāgni-

yo vṛṣṭikāmaḥ sa saubhareṇa stuvīteti prakṛtastotrāśrayatvaṃ saubharasya |

prakaraṇaprāptayoranuvādo vaktavyaḥ | saubhareṇeti tu padārtho vidhīyate |
na cāsau kriyāntaramanapekṣya kārakaṃ bhavatīti tadapekṣā | atra tu karotyartha-
tu

1
| ૨૪૨
yāgaṃ hi phale vidadhat pratyayo na viprakṛṣyate | na cānutpattiparaḥ
sannutpattimapi kalpayiṣyati | anekārthavidhānaṃ ca yathaivotpattividhī karmavidhāna-
nimittaṃ labhyate, tathā phalavidhāvapi phaloddeśena karmavidhānādityevaṃ manyamāno
vadati - atha revatīṣu kṛtena vāravantīyena paśukāmo yajeteti | stotradvāreṇa
yāgasādhanatvaṃ papatteḥ karaṇatṛtīyavaiṣā | aya vetthaṃbhūtalakṣaṇā | anenaivetthaṃbhūtena
paśukāmo yajeteti | tatrottaram | naivaṃ śakyam, ṛgantarapramāṇā dviśeṣa
hānādvaiguṇyamiti | yadi tadevedaṃ karma tatastasya vāyavyāsvagniṣṭomasāma vihitaṃ
tadṛgantare revatyākhye gīyamānaṃ pūrvavihitavāyavyākhyaviśeṣahānā dviguṇaṃ bhavet |
nanvidānīmevātmīye pakṣe bhavataiva tatra revatīṣu ṛkṣvanyānyapi sāmāni
prāpnuvantyevaṃ paryanuyuktenābhihitam | naiṣa doṣaḥ | kṛtveti nivṛttiḥ saṃbandho
yāgādhyocyate | dvāvetāvarthau kṛtvetyeṣa śabdaḥ śaknoti vaditumiti | siddhānta-
vādī tvapūrvakarmavidhāvanekārthavidhānaṃ śakyaṃ nānyatreti manyamāna āha-
sati vacane matpakṣe śakyaṃ bhavatpakṣe tvasati vāravantīyagrahaṇamātreṇa labhya-
mānāsu revatīṣvaśvayamiti | paraḥ punarāha - vacanaṃ tahi bhaviṣyatīti |
tenaivābhiprāyeṇa | phale viśiṣṭa karmavidhānātkṛtvāśabdasya cāvidhāyakatvāt |
yajiranuvāda iti ca satyapi phalaṃ prati vidhātumutpattyavidhānābhiprāyeṇa |
siddhāntavādī strābhipretānekārthaviṣyanyathānupapattiphalaṃ kathayati yadi
vacanaṃ siddhaṃ tarhi karmāntaraṃ na pūrvasyaivāgniṣṭuto guṇavidhānamiti |
nanu tato yajeteti yāgānuvādāditi vacanenānirākṛtatvādadyāpi sva.
pakṣameva balīyāṃsaṃ manyate | naivaṃ śakyamiti siddhāntavivaraṇam | yadi
hi revatīguṇakaṃ vāravantīyaṃ yāgaṃ prati guṇatvena vidhīyate tato'vaśyaṃ
sa uddeṣṭavyaḥ | tataścoddiśyamānatvādeva phalapadaṃ na tena guṇena vā
saṃbadhyata ityanarthakameva syātsaṃbadhyamānaṃ vā vākyaṃ bhindyāt | guṇaviśiṣṭa-
karmavidhāne vāpratyabhijñāyamānatvādekāntena karmāntaratvam | na ca yatpaśukāmo
yajetetyaprāptatvādanuvādaḥ saṃbhavati | etena revatīvāravattīyavidhi pratyasya
nimittatvaṃ pratyuktam | ataśca na vāravantīyaṃ kenacid balavattareṇa pramāṇena
revatībhiḥ saṃbaddhamiti viśeṣahānād viguṇaṃ bhavet | atha yāgasaṃbandho'nuvāda
iti | guṇādeva phale sati prakaraṇādavagataṃ ya'gāṅgatvamasmin vākye'nuvadiṣyata
iti | kaḥ punarāśrayāśrayisaṃbandhātpūrvanirākṛtādasya viśeṣo yenopanyasyate |
1. yāgasya iti mudrita pustaka ṭippaṇīdhṛtapāṭhaḥ |

māne kriyāntarasyāgniṣṭomasāmno viśeṣaṇena kathaṃ saṃbandha iti | tatrottaraṃ
vacanāditi | prāguktanyāyenātideśikaṃ vacanaṃ draṣṭavyam | agniṣṭomasāmnaḥ

tasmātsameṣu karmayuktaṃ phalaṃ syāditi siddham || 27||
saubharādhikaraṇam
jyotiṣṭome lobharamukthyānāṃ brahmasāma vihitam | punaśca tadeva 'yadi
rathantarasāma' yadi vṛhatsāma' iti ca nimittayovihitam | tataḥ punaḥ 'yo

syaiva sataḥ phalatrayasaṃbandhaḥ kṛtaḥ | tathā ca vakṣyati evaṃ jātīyakeṣu sa
phalaṃ yadanekārthavidhinā siddhe nānyathānupapattyā svopapādakamapūrvakarma-

na kiñcidagniṣṭometi | yadeva tatsādhanaṃ tadevāgniṣṭomasāma | tena

nyāyasiddhaṃ kratvarthasyāpi vṛṣṭyādiphalatvaṃ tacca paryāyeṇeti darśayati---

na śabdekatvena yatkiñcitkārye sthānaṃ dṛṣṭasāmarthyaṃ tadevānyatrāpīti
śakyate vaktum | na hi yad devadattasya yudhyamānasya sthānamavagataṃ tadeva
bhuñjānasyāpi bhavatīti gamyate | kāryaprayuktā hi sthānaviśeṣādayo na

tasyaiva sthānaviśeṣalābhāt prāgasiddhatvāt | itaretarāśrayaṃ hi syāt sobhara -

bhedaka ityuktam | iha ca bahuvrīhyarthaṃ sadbhāvasya yadiśabdopavandhena nimittatvaṃ
gamyate na rathantarasattāyā upasarjanatvāt | na ca kvatuviśiṣṭaṃ rathantaramiha
pratīyate | yena tadeva nimittaṃ kalpyeta | na ca tat kratunāvacchettuṃ śakyate,
anyatrāpi vidyamānatvāt | evaṃ hi tadavacchidyate yadi rathantaramasyaiva sāmeti,
na tvevaṃ saṃbhavati | tasmādavaśyamevaṃ kartavyaṃ yadi rathantaramevāsya sāmeti |
na caivamātmakatvaṃ jyotiṣṭomasyānyasya vāvagataṃ, yannimittaṃ bhavet | bhato
nimittapratīti parityajya tadanyathānupapattyaiva kevalarathantarasāmakarmāntaravidhi -
radhyavasīyate | śakyaṃ hi tadapūrvaṃ kartum | na ca rathantarasattāmātraṃ viśeṣaṇa-

padadvayaṃ vidhāyakaṃ, grahān gṛhṇīyādityetattu yadyapyavidhāyakaṃ tathāpīcchārthatvād
yo hi yadicchati sa tatkarotītyevaṃ vidhiphalaṃ lapsyate | atha vā icchāmātra
yuktamupāttaṃ grahāgratvaṃ syādityeṣaiva liṅ vidhāsyati |
vinaiva vyavahitakalpanayāpyayaṃ pakṣo bhavan balavattara iti manvāno'tha vā
hetuhetumatorliṅityupanyasyati | tatra somaḥ syāditi hetī liṅa, gṛhṇīyāditi
vidhāveva | tenaitaduktaṃ bhavati rathantarasāmasoma hetukānaṃndravāyavāgrān grahān
grahaṇīyāditi | na cākriyamāṇe'sī hetutvaṃ pratipadyata ityarthādvihito bhaviṣya-
tīti | atha vā hetumati gṛhṇīyāditi liṅ, somaḥ syāditi vidhāvanuktamapi ca
somasya hetutvaṃ hetumadākṣepātsaṃnniveśca bhaviṣyati | tatrāpyeṣo'rtho grahāgratāviśeṣa
kāraṇabhūtaḥ somayāgaḥ kartavya iti |
yadiśabdaparityāgo rūḍhyadhyāhārakalpanā |
vyavadhānena saṃbandho hetuhetumatośca liṅ ||
iti pakṣavikalpa saṃgrahaślokaḥ | sarvaṃ caitadavaiṣṭyadhikaraṇa siddhānta evopa-
yokṣyata ityatra prapañcitam | kiñca-
niṣpannena nimittena niṣpādyaṃ pratibhūyate |
na rathagtarasattā ca niṣpannā kvatusattvavat ||
prātaḥ savane hi grahāgratā bhavati mādhyandine rathantaram, na cāniṣpa-
nnasya nimittatā yuktā | kāraṇaparyāyatvānnimittaśabdasya, mama tu naiva kiñci-
siddhinaṃ viparītam | tamupasecanena bhojanaṃ prayujyateti | yo hi yadīcchatīti |
pathā yāgānuṣṭhānāveva svargaḥ kṛto bhavati naivaṃ mahānatvaṃ kintu pṛthak tasmin
pravṛttau tena tavicchāto yāgakriyopadiśyata iti | tadetatkathaṃ bhavati |
yadi yāge pravṛtte tasminnapi pravartayitavyaṃ bhavatīti | atha vecchāmātrayuktamiti
grahāgratā kriyāmicchato yāgopadeśo yāgaprayuktatāṃ tasya bodhayannaṅgabhāvaṃ
gamayatīti | tataśca sāṅgayāgavidhinā vidhiriti gṛhṇīyāditi |
vidhāveveti | atrānuktamapyasya hetumatvahetvākṣepātsannidheśca bhaviṣyatīti |
athavā hetumatīti | kathamidānīṃ yadi śabdaḥ | taducyate vihitasya parāmarśakaḥ |
yadyevaṃ tadā grahāgrateti | atha vā kraturityaṅgitvādaṅgānuṣṭhāne nimittamiti |

bhavati bhaviṣyati ceti | tatrehārthavaśenaivaṃ vijñāyate yadi rathantaraṃ bhaviṣyatīti |

tasya viṣuvato nimittattvena grahaṇam | tasmānnimittārthāni sarvāṇi śravaṇā-
nīti ||2||

'rājā rājasūyena svārājyakāmo yajeta' ityasya prakaraṇe 'āgneyo'ṣṭā-
