uta rājyakaraṇanimitta iti | nanu ca 'etayānnādyakāmaṃ yājayet' iti varṇaviśe-
ṣaṇāsaṃbaddhaṃ pṛthagrājasūyaprayogaṃ vacanādevāveṣṭervacanamastīti varṇatrayasyāpi prāptasya
atha vā sādhanabhedādasti tatkṛtaḥ sāṅgapradhānaprayogabhedaḥ | tatra prayogaprakane
vaikalpikamanavadhārayatāṃ na kaścitprayogaḥ prakramya sādhitaḥ syāt |
avadhārayata eva tu nirūpitaikaprayogasiddhiḥ | kauśalenābhyāso yathoktaḥ |
ādiśabdena vaikalpikasambandheṣvanuṣṭhāneṣu bahūnāṃ sampratipattiruktā |
tadyathā atraiva rathantarāvadhāraṇe tannimittāyāmaindravāyavāgratāyāmadhvaryudgātṛ-
hotṛṇāṃ pragṛhītṛstotṛyājyānuvākyāprayoktṛṇāmiti | adhikāraviśeṣaṇabhūta
iti | jātivacanatthādrājaśabdo nādhikāraviśeṣaṇaṃ sambhavati | tathā hi
apravṛttapravartako vidhirudāsīnamanāgate pravartayati | tena kādācitkī
kartavyatā vidhyarthaḥ | samprati pravartata saṃvaṃrūpā kādācitkena nimittena
sambadhyate, na nityena adhikāraviśeṣaṇamapi ca tasmin satyadhikārā-
nimitte'ntarbhavati | yathā jīvanaṃ prāṇadhāraṇa kriyāpratikṣaṇamupajāyamānā
pratyekaṃ nimittaṃ bhavati | evaṃ jātiḥ pratikṣaṇaṃ na ! jāyate nāsti vā |
janmamaraṇāntarāle vartamānatvāt | tasmādevaṃ vyākhyā | kartuṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ
rājapadaṃ rājakartṛ kastu prayogo jāteranupādeyatvānna rājñā śakyate kartum |
aśaktasya cānadhikāraḥ | tenārthādrājñe' vagamādadhikāraviśeṣaṇaṃ rājaśabda
ityucyate | tadetad vyaktīkariṣyati | rājakartṛ katvavaśena bhoktṛviśeṣa
iti | nanu caitayeti kathaṃ prāptānāṃ karmaṇāṃ phalasambandhaṃ katṛsambandhaṃ caikaṃ

sā tu brāhmaṇaparivrājakanyāyena rājanyaśabdasyābhiṣikta viśeṣagāmitvena sāmarthya-
labhyatvāt | yattu prasiddhidvayasaṃbhave rājyanimittatvaṃ prakaraṇādibhyo vijñāsyata
iti tannirākkriyate | tathā hi-
naiva tāvadubho śabdo svātantryeṇa prasiddhyataḥ |
klṛptāyāmekaśaktī hi dvitīyo yaugiko varam ||
tadetaddarśayati-yadi tāvajjātiśabdo rājeti tatastasya karma rājyamitya-
vayavaprasiddhisaṃbhavānna tannirapekṣaṃ śaktyantarakalpanamupapadyate | vinaiva tenāryāvarta -
nivāsināṃ prayogo na virotsyate | atha tu prathamameva rājyamiti pratipālanaṃ
grahaṇam | yadetadvākyaviṣayīkṛtaṃ karma dvārarājeti | yattu svacchandenāvaidhaṃ
kriyate, tadvidhīnāmupalakṣitatvādadhruvamanimittaṃ śabdapravṛtteriti | kathaṃ
sāmānyasambandhe'bhiṣiktaviśeṣagāmitvamiti cedata āha- sāmarthya labdha-
tvāditi | rājanyānāṃ yuddham upadiśya viśeṣavacanaṃ na rājā rājānaṃ
hagyāditi | tadrājaśabdasya viśeṣagāmitve viśeṣaṇañca saṃskāra viśeṣa-
stadvidhau ghaṭate rājaśabdasya tatsāhacaryadarśanādrājaṃśabdavaditi | yattu
prasiddhidvayeti | yaduktaṃ bhavedeva jātiśabdo'yaṃ yaugiko'pi tatra ca yaugika-
grahaṇamatreti | naiva tāvadubhāviti | rājyaśabdasya rūḍhitve syādapi tadyogātsa
eva tu yaugika iti | taddhitasya kita iti vihitaḥ pratyayasvaro'tra pratīyate

carudvāreṇeti | nirvāpasya carupuroḍāśārthasya tayoryāgārthatāyāṃ yāgārthatā

bhavatīti | na cālambhanyāyena siddheranārambha ityāha- devatāśaṅkā cātreti |
carutvādyāgārthatvaṃ pratīyata ityuktaṃ yāgekadeśastu ātmīyatvaparityāgo vidhā-
nādeva siddho nahi svayaṃ yatheṣṭaviniyogārthe carau vidhikṛtyamasti | atadarthe
cāprabhoḥ puruṣasya na 'svātantryamityāha - sarvathā caitasyeti | aṅgaprāpti vidhā-

[ 293
kāṅkṣite yogyatvādyāgopayogitvameva vijñāyate | vākyaśeṣe ca bṛhaspatiḥ śrūya-

prāptam | evaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ |

yathāpramāṇamevaiṣāṃ viniyogo'vagamyate ||
'nevāraścarurbhavati' iti ha, yutpannamātrasyaiva prayojanāpekṣāyāṃ satyāṃ yāgā-
ṅgabhūtāgnidhāraṇārthaṃ sthala nivṛttiśeṣatvena pratyakṣavidhānaṃ dṛśyate 'yadenaṃ caru-
mupadadhāti' iti | tathā nirākāṅkṣasya ca caroḥ śrutenāpi kāryāntareṇa
saṃbandho na syāt kimutāśrutenaiva yāgena | na ceyaṃ pratipattiriti prathamaṃ prayojana-
vattvānavagamāt | arthakarmaṇyapi copadhānaṃ prati prādhānyamasti, tenāsaṃskṛtasthalaṃ
na nirvartayatīti dvitīyānirdeśāvirodhaḥ |

[ pātnīvatādhikaraṇam | ]
etenaivādhikāreṇotsṛjatirapi yajimadabhidhāno na veti vicāryate |

[ prakaraṇāntarādhikaraṇam ]
gataṃ guṇalakṣaṇabhedaprasaktānuprasaktaṃ kīdṛśo guṇaḥ pūrvatrāsaṃbhavan bhinatti
kīdṛśo vā tatraiva saṃbhavatīti | idānīṃ paryavasiteṣu pañcasu bhedakāraṇeṣu
prakaraṇāntaralakṣaṇo bhedaḥ prastūyate | tatra tāvat
agnihotrādiśabdena pūrvaṃ karmāvabodhitam |
na viprakarṣamātreṇa bhedarūpaṃ prapadyate |
yathaiva balabhyāṃ pāṭaliputre vāgnihotrādiśabda uccāryamāṇo nārthāntaraṃ
pratipādayati, evaṃ prakaraṇāntare'pi | na hi prakaraṇāntaraṃ tasyārthābhidhānaśakti-
mabhivyanakti | tadviśeṣitāśca yajatijuhotyādayo'pi nārthāntare vartante |
nārthāntareṇa vinā kiñcidanarthakaṃ bhavati | na ca prāptavidhānaṃ teṣu na saṃbhavati |
yāvajjovādividhibhiḥ saha tulyabalatvena vikalpopapatteḥ | yo vai teṣāṃ kāmya-
prayogastasyānavagatakāla viśeṣatvānmāsaparimāṇa vidhāna mityadoṣaḥ | prakaraṇasya ca
vākyaṃ bāghakamiṣṭameva | atha vā dīkṣitatvanimittena pratiṣiddhānāmapi māsa-
parimitaḥ pratiprasavaḥ | sa tu prayogamadhya eva kriyate | atha vā tānyevāgni-
hotrādīni atra paryupasthāpya somayāgasahitāni sattraprayogavacanena phale
vidhīyante | na hi kevalabhojī devadatto'nyaiḥ saha paṅktyāṃ bhuñjāno'nyatvaṃ

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
yadi hi māsaḥ śuddho vidhīyeta tataḥ pūrvakarmānuvāde puddhirbhavedapi | na
tvetadasti | upasaduttarakālatvena śravaṇādvidyamānābhirūpasadbhiḥ śakyaḥ kālo-
lakṣayitum | na ca naiyamike tāḥ svārthāḥ parārthāḥ vā vidyante | yadi tu tāstaduttara-
kālatvaṃ māsaśca vidhīyeran, evaṃ sati anekārthavidhānādvākyabhedaḥ syāt |
etasmādeva hetordīkṣitadāna homapākapratiṣedhena pratiṣiddhasya pratiprasavarūpeṇa
vā satraikadeśatvena vāyaṃ vidhiḥ pratyuktaḥ | tatrāpi hyupasaduttarakālatvaṃ māsaścetya-
nekārthatvameva | karmāntiravidhāne tvanekārthatvamadoṣaḥ | tasmātkarmāntaramiti
bhāṣyakāraḥ | idaṃ tviha vaktavyam | yadi vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt karmāntaratvamevaṃ
tahi sa eva bhedahetuḥ prāpnoti na prakaraṇāntaram | atha vā guṇādevānekātma-
katvena pūrvatrāsaṃbhavād bheda iti prakaraṇāntarānavakāśaḥ | tathā hi
kiñca-

uttareṣāṃ tu naiva syādupasatsaṃgavinā ||

[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
vidhyaśaktereṣā vacanavyaktirbhavati tatraiva karma kartavyamiti | paroddeśena ca karma
vidhīyamānaṃ yadyanyena tadrūpameva vākyenopasthāpyate tatastadevetyakarmāntara buddhi-
bhavati | atha tvīṣadapi tirodhānānnopatiṣṭheta tataḥ paraṃ vidhīyamānatvenaiva
vihitādanyatvabuddhiḥ sthitā na śakyā'panetumiti karmāntaratvaṃ bhavati | evaṃ hi
sa vidhāyakastatropadiśati avidheyaṃ padārtha prati mayaitatkarma vidhīyate | tacca
svarūpeṇānutpannaṃ na śakyaṃ tatra vidhātumiti balādutpādyate | tadyadi kaści-
devaṃvidhaṃ śaknotyutpādayituṃ tata utpādayatu, yāvadahamapyatra vidhi karomi |
atha notpādayati tato maya~votpādite mā kaścitpaścādāgatya mamāparādhaṃ maṃsteti |
tenaivamācchoṣite yadi kaścittathāvidhamupanayati tato'sāvapi tadeva deśādī
vidhatte | atha nopanayati tataḥ sa evotpādayatyapīti nirūpyate | tadiha
māsastāvad dūrasthahomānuvādena na śakyate vidhātum | na hi kaściddhomādyanu-
rodhenādyaiva māsaṃ saṃpādayituṃ samarthastenātyantaṃ svatantramanupādeyaṃ māsameva
pratihomādiṣu vidhīyamāneṣu dūrasthairutpattividhibhirutpādanaśaktāvalupyamānā-
L
vyāpārātsambandho na svatantrasyeti | yaddhi yathāsiddhasyājahaddeśasya
svatantrasyetarasambandhārthamabhidhānam | sa uddeśaḥ | sa ca deśakāla -
yorarthasvabhāvānnimittasya nimittatvādeva | phalasaṃskāryayoḥ pratisambandhi-
guṇabhāvaparatvādvākyasya | tenaibhiḥ sambandhe pharma eṣā vacanavyaktistatraiva
karma kartavyamiti | anupādeyatantratā ghopādeyānuṣṭhānasyātra saptamyartho'nā-
dhārataiva phalanimittayorasambhavāt | tataśca śabdeveti yatra tatrasthaṃ karmaviṣaya-
tvenāṅgī' kṛtaṃ kintu tatraitra kartavyamityatra dvāvaṃśau karma tasya cetarasambandhaḥ
tatra yadi yathopāttaṃ karma buddhau viparivartate tathā buddheranatiriktārtha
tvāditarasambandhātiriktena vākyasya sāphalyāgnobhayārthatāṃ gophiyata itya-
karmāntaratā | viparivṛtyabhāve tu apūrva buddhakarmabhireveyamavatiṣṭhate | śaknoti
hi pratisambandhinaṃ uddeśyatvātkarmā ropitavyāpāraḥ śabdastasyānyasambandha-
vadutpattimapi kartumityuktam paraṃ pratividhīyamānatveneti | na ca śabda-
pravṛttisamaye'nupasthānādbhedabuddhirupajātā paścāddūrasthasyānusaṃdhānena parā-
vartate tasya śabdenānaṅgīkṛtatvāditi darśātaṃ na śakyamapanetumiti | tadetad-
vyaktīkaroti - evaṃ hi | tatreti | tataśceti | nāmadheyasyāpyutpatyupasthānenā-

yāmidānīmevotpattiḥ kriyate | na cotpannasya punaruktiḥ 1 saṃbhavatītyapūrvavidhānaṃ
vijñāyate | nanu, cāgnihotrādipadenotpattisahacāriṇā tatkarmotpattāvapanīya-
mānāyāṃ māsasaṃbandhamātravyāpṛto'yaṃ vidhirutpādakatvādvihanyeta |

ucyate |

karmopasthāpyate tatra tadevetyavasīyate |
saṃnidhānena hi yādṛgeva deśādivākyeṣu karmāvagamyate tādṛgevopatiṣṭhata
iti na bhidyate | nāmadheyaṃ punarāditastāvadākhyāta parantratvānnaiva bhedābhedayo-

bhāvanā tvanupasthānādbhidyamānāvadhāryate ||

durbalamapyabhimatasampādakamaṅgīkriyate | na durbalatvenābhibhūyata ityarthaḥ |

anavasthitamānatvād godohādiṣu rūpataḥ |
[ dvitīyādhyāyasya
cihna vyavasthayecchanti nityaṃ sūryādiceṣṭitam ||
kiyatā kālena gaurdahyata ityukte niyatānyeva muhūrtādīni pariccheda-
katvenopādīyante | tatparicchedaśca nāḍikālakṣaṇalavatruṭinimeṣādibhirvyāva-
hārikakviyāvyatiriktaireva kriyate | tadabhyāsavaśena tu vyāvahārikāṇāmapi
prāyikaparimāṇajñānātparicchedakatvaṃ bhavati | na ca jīvanaṃ nāma nimeṣādivanni
yataparimāṇaṃ yena paricchaṃdakatvasāmarthyātkālatvenāśrīyate | tasmādvyatirikta-
kālalakṣaṇā duṣpariharā | punaścābhyāsalakṣaṇā | katham |
prakṛtakriyāṇāṃ yāvāntarakriyā tatparimāṇena dohenārthatvopalakṣyamāṇā-
stānasāmastyena kintvavāntarakriyāparimāṇaṃ yattenāvacchinnā lakṣyante |
kimityevaṃ kalpyata iti cetahi tatrāha -- anavasthitamānatvāditi | nahi
dohaḥ svamahimnāvacchedakaḥ sambhavati | yataḥ svayamevāsāvanavasthitamāna-
tvādanyāpekṣaḥ | nahi kriyālakṣaṇayeyattayā niyatakriyotpādakaphaleyattayā
